### [95] [Inference-Time Orthogonal Seeding Enables Geometry-Aligned 3D Organ Segmentation for Slice-Propagation Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12658)
*Md Rakibul Haque,Tushar Kataria,Shireen Y. Elhabian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对3D医学图像分割中密集体素标注的瓶颈问题，提出使用三个正交种子切片（轴向、冠状、矢状各一个）替代单一轴向种子进行切片传播分割。研究发现训练范式影响有限，性能主要取决于推理时的种子几何结构（正交性而非种子数量），正交播种配合轴向训练的Sli2Vol骨干网络在Dice、Normalized Surface Dice和Average Hausdorff Distance上分别提升21.9%、25.5%和53.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D医学图像分割需要密集体素级标注，成本高昂。现有的单切片传播方法（如Sli2Vol）通过无标签配准从一个标注的种子切片向整卷传播，但仅使用轴向切片会忽略冠状和矢状信息，导致距离种子越远误差累积越严重，尤其影响表面距离指标，未能充分利用CT/MRI的3D信息。

Method: 系统研究了影响切片传播模型的关键训练和推理选择，包括单轴与多轴无标签配准、单种子与多种子传播、正交种子配置。提出使用三个正交种子（轴向、冠状、矢状各一个）进行传播，并通过简单的无标签融合规则合并传播标签。比较了轴向训练网络应用于离轴种子与显式三轴训练等不同路径。

Result: 在多种器官CT数据集上，使用正交播种与轴向Sli2Vol骨干网络相比单轴基线：Dice提升21.9%，Normalized Surface Dice提升25.5%，Average Hausdorff Distance降低53.5%。训练范式影响有限，轴向训练网络应用于离轴种子即可获得绝大部分改进，显式三轴训练收益甚微；性能主要由推理时种子的正交性驱动，种子数量本身并非关键因素。

Conclusion: 推理时的种子几何结构（正交性）是提升切片传播模型性能的核心因素，而非训练范式或种子数量。仅通过调整推理策略（采用正交种子配置）即可在不改变训练流程的情况下大幅提升分割精度，为3D医学图像分割提供了一种简单有效的改进范式。

Abstract: Dense voxel-level annotation remains a major bottleneck in 3D medical image segmentation. Single-slice propagation methods such as Sli2Vol reduce this burden by propagating one annotated seed slice through a volume using label-free registration. However, axial-only propagation accumulates errors with distance from the seed, especially in surface-distance metrics, because it ignores coronal and sagittal evidence and therefore underuses the 3D information available in CT/MRI volumes. To better leverage volumetric geometry, we study how key training and inference choices affect slice-propagation models, including single-axis versus multi-axis label-free registration, single-seed versus multi-seed propagation, and orthogonal seed configurations. Instead of propagating from a single axial seed, we use three orthogonal seeds---one axial, one coronal, and one sagittal---and fuse their propagated labels with a simple label-free rule. Our results show that the training paradigm has limited impact: an axially trained network applied to off-axis seeds captures nearly all the improvement, while explicit three-axis training adds little. Instead, performance is driven by inference-time seed geometry, especially orthogonality rather than the number of annotated slices, as a budget-matched three-axial control provides no benefit and can even degrade performance. On a multi-organ CT cohort, orthogonal seeding with the axial Sli2Vol backbone improves Dice by 21.9%, Normalized Surface Dice by 25.5%, and reduces Average Hausdorff Distance by 53.5% over the single-axis baseline.

</details>


### [96] [Mr3D-VL: A generalist vision language foundation model for Multiparametric 3D Magnetic Resonance Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12689)
*Zhi Qiao,Xintong Wu,Yichu He,Feng Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Mr3D-VL，一个40亿参数的多参数3D MRI专用视觉-语言基础模型。它采用无监督预训练的共享3D编码器和4D旋转位置嵌入实现模态-空间双重整合，并通过多分辨率特征植入策略的跨模态投影层增强特征感知。在文本生成任务上显著优于现有4B/7B/30B通用及领域专属模型，报告生成BERTScore达0.856，问答准确率0.713，选择题准确率0.912。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑肿瘤mpMRI的AI模型缺乏自然语言交互和可解释性，难以进行空间信息整合和跨模态推理。关键挑战包括：模态间物理含义差异大、扫描间隔导致的空间错位、以及胶质瘤分级等任务需要复杂多特征解释。现有VLM多聚焦2D图像建模，缺乏对3D体积空间的直接感知；虽已有3D VLM用于CT报告生成，但mpMRI需要多模态协同推理，现有方案无法满足。

Method: Mr3D-VL采用40亿参数架构，包含三个核心设计：(1)无监督预训练的共享3D编码器，用于统一多模态特征提取；(2)4D旋转位置嵌入实现模态与空间的双重整合，解决多模态错位问题；(3)跨模态投影层采用多分辨率特征植入策略，增强不同分辨率下的特征感知能力。

Result: 在文本生成任务中显著优于现有4B/7B/30B规模的领域专属和通用模型。报告生成BERTScore达到0.856，问题回答准确率0.713，多项选择准确率0.912。

Conclusion: Mr3D-VL作为首个面向多参数3D MRI的视觉-语言基础模型，有效解决了多模态协同推理和3D空间感知问题，在脑肿瘤诊断相关的文本生成任务上取得显著性能提升。

Abstract: Multi-parametric magnetic resonance imaging (mpMRI) is a cornerstone for brain tumor diagnosis and treatment, yet current AI models face critical limitations: their lack of natural language interaction and interpretability impedes spatial information integration and cross-modal reasoning required clinically. Key challenges arise from significant physical meaning differences across modalities, spatial misalignment due to scan intervals, and the need for complex multi-feature interpretation in tasks like glioma grading. While visual-language models (VLMs) show promise in cross-modal understanding, existing methods focus mainly on 2D image modeling, neglecting direct perception of 3D volumetric space. Although 3D VLMs have been proposed for report generation and feature alignment in 3D CT imaging, mpMRI applications demand collaborative inference across multiple imaging modalities-a requirement unmet by current solutions. To address this, we introduce Mr3D-VL, a dedicated visual-language foundation model for multi-parametric 3D MRI. With 4 billion parameters, it employs an unsupervised pre-trained shared 3D encoder and 4D rotational positional embedding for dual modality-spatial integration. Its cross-modal projection layer uses a multi-resolution feature implantation strategy to enhance feature perception across resolutions. Experimental results show significant improvements over existing 4B/7B/30B domain-specific and general-purpose models in text generation tasks, achieving a BERTScore of 0.856 for report generation, with question-answering accuracy at 0.713 and multiple-choice accuracy at 0.912.

</details>


### [97] [Class Geometry as Supervision for Sample-Efficient Open-World Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12698)
*Akash Rao,Zhou Chen,Revanth Reddy Palem,Udhav Ramachandran,Ruth Scimeca,Sathyanarayanan N. Aakur*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出类几何监督（CGS）框架，通过约束学习到的原形或类表示空间，使其保留训练数据中估计的视觉或语义类间相似度/差异度结构，从而提升开放世界目标检测在数据稀缺场景（如生物医学成像）下的性能，包括样本效率、新颖类插入和开放集检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界目标检测要求模型识别已知类、拒绝未知类并随时间内化新类，在数据稀缺场景（如生物医学和科学成像）中尤其具有挑战性。基于原形的检测器虽是自然选择，但通常将类原形视为独立锚点，忽略类间关系结构，限制了它们在稀缺数据下的泛化和可扩展能力。

Method: 提出类几何监督（CGS）通用框架，引入保持差异度的目标函数，使学习到的类表示之间的成对距离对齐到目标类几何矩阵，同时保留标准任务损失。该目标函数在同一框架下实例化于原型识别、少样本生物医学目标检测、开放集检测、新类插入以及COCO上的OWOD适应等多种场景。

Result: 实验表明CGS提升了识别和开放式目标检测（ova detection）的样本效率，显著增强了新类插入能力，并在COCO上提高了未知类召回率，同时保留了大部分已知类检测性能。消融实验显示，有意义的视觉几何提供最可靠的增益，而随机几何虽有助于新类分离但在少样本检测上一致性较差。

Conclusion: 关系类几何是一种有效的监督信号，可在有限监督下构建校准且可扩展的开放世界检测器，为数据稀缺场景下的开放世界目标检测提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Open-world object detection requires models to recognize known categories, reject unfamiliar objects, and incorporate new classes over time. This is especially challenging in scarce-data settings such as biomedical and scientific imaging, where rare categories may have only a few annotated examples and fine-grained classes differ by subtle morphology. Prototype-based detectors are natural for this regime, but they typically learn class prototypes as independent anchors, ignoring relational structure among classes. We propose class-geometry supervision (CGS), a general framework that constrains learned prototype or class-representation spaces to preserve visual or semantic class dissimilarities estimated from training data. CGS introduces a dissimilarity-preserving objective that aligns pairwise distances among learned class representations with a target class-geometry matrix while retaining the standard task loss. We instantiate the same objective across prototype recognition, few-shot biomedical object detection, open-set detection, novel-class insertion, and OWOD adaptation on COCO. Experiments show that CGS improves sample efficiency in recognition and ova detection, substantially strengthens novel-class insertion, and improves unknown recall on COCO while retaining much of the known-class detection performance. Ablations show that meaningful visual geometry provides the most reliable gains, while random geometry can help novel separation but is less consistent for few-shot detection. These results suggest that relational class geometry is an effective supervisory signal for building calibrated and extensible open-world detectors under limited supervision.

</details>


### [98] [Towards Sparsely Annotated Open-World Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12714)
*HeeJu Han,AJeong Kim,Jinsun Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出稀疏标注开放世界目标检测（SA-OWOD）新任务，并设计统一框架DPOD，通过已知目标恢复模块（KTRM）和双分歧目标生成器（DDTG）联合处理稀疏监督与未知类别共存问题，在基准测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的目标检测面临模糊监督问题：未标注区域既可能是已知对象的缺失标注，也可能是真正未知的类别。现有的稀疏标注目标检测（SAOD）和开放世界目标检测（OWOD）分别解决了这两个挑战，但它们在实践中的共现问题尚未被解决。

Method: 提出双视角目标发现框架（DPOD），包含两个互补机制：已知目标恢复模块（KTRM）恢复未标注已知实例的监督信号，并显式正则化特征空间以分离已知与未知表征；双分歧目标生成器（DDTG）通过跨视角语义不一致性识别可靠的未知候选目标。通过整合这两个模块，缓解模糊未标注区域导致的矛盾监督信号。

Result: 在稀疏标注开放世界基准上的实验表明，该方法优于现有开放世界检测方法，尤其在未知目标检测方面表现突出，同时防止已知与未知对象之间的误分类并稳定决策边界。

Conclusion: DPOD通过联合建模未标注的已知和未知实例，有效解决了稀疏监督与未知类别共存这一开放问题，为真实场景下的开放世界目标检测提供了统一解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world object detection operates under ambiguous supervision, where unlabeled regions may correspond to missing annotations of known objects or genuinely unknown categories. These challenges have been addressed separately in Sparsely Annotated Object Detection (SAOD) and Open-World Object Detection (OWOD). In practice, their co-occurrence remains an open problem. To address this problem, we introduce Sparsely Annotated Open-World Object Detection (SA-OWOD), a new task that jointly considers sparse supervision and the presence of unseen categories. We propose Dual-Perspective Object Discovery (DPOD), a unified framework that jointly models unlabeled known and unknown instances via two complementary mechanisms. The Known Target Recovery Module (KTRM) recovers supervision for unlabeled known instances and explicitly regularizes the feature space to separate known and unknown representations. Complementarily, the Dual-Disagreement Target Generator (DDTG) identifies reliable unknown candidates through cross-view semantic inconsistency. By integrating these modules, DPOD resolves contradictory supervision signals caused by ambiguous unlabeled regions. As a result, it prevents misclassification between known and unknown objects and stabilizes the decision boundaries. Experimental results on sparsely annotated open-world benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms existing open-world detection methods, particularly in detecting unknown objects.

</details>


### [99] [VOS-Agent: The 1st Place Solution for the 8th LSVOS Challenge (MOSEv2 Track)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12721)
*Canyang Wu,Jinrong Zhang,Xusheng He,Ce Bian,Xianjing Han,Jianlong Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VOS-Agent是一个协作式视频目标分割框架，以SAM3作为共享的密集分割模块，根据目标特征条件性地激活专门代理（路由代理、视觉跟踪代理、基于MLLM的语义代理），针对微小目标和语义主导目标分别提供专用路径，在MOSEv2测试集上达到69.82%的J&F指标，并在ECCV 2026第八届LSVOS挑战赛MOSEv2赛道中排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂视频目标分割需要在严重遮挡、消失与重现情况下进行稳健的目标传播。尽管SAM3提供了强大的可提示掩膜传播能力，但统一的推理路径对两类目标不可靠：一是视觉证据不足的微小目标，二是身份依赖于显式属性的语义主导目标。

Method: 提出VOS-Agent协作框架，保留SAM3作为共享的密集分割模块，并基于目标特征条件性激活专门代理。具体包括三个组件：1) 目标感知与路由代理（Target Perception and Routing Agent）将每个序列分配到常规、微小或语义主导路径；2) 视觉跟踪代理（Visual Tracking Agent）通过置信度感知的框提示支持微小目标；3) 基于MLLM的语义代理（MLLM-based Semantic Agent）通过描述引导的定位和候选验证处理语义主导目标。

Result: 在MOSEv2测试集上，VOS-Agent在官方J&F指标上达到69.82%，并在ECCV 2026第八届LSVOS挑战赛MOSEv2赛道中排名第一。

Conclusion: 通过条件化路由和专业化代理的协作，VOS-Agent有效解决了统一推理路径在微小目标和语义主导目标上的不可靠性问题，显著提升了复杂场景下视频目标分割的鲁棒性，并在权威基准测试中取得了领先成绩。

Abstract: Complex video object segmentation requires robust target propagation under severe occlusion, disappearance and reappearance. Although SAM3 provides strong promptable mask propagation, a uniform inference path remains unreliable for tiny targets with insufficient visual evidence and semantic-dominated targets whose identities depend on explicit attributes. To this end, we present VOS-Agent, a collaborative framework that retains SAM3 as the shared dense segmentation module and conditionally activates specialized agents according to target characteristics. A Target Perception and Routing Agent assigns each sequence to a regular, tiny, or semantic-dominated route. Tiny targets are supported by a Visual Tracking Agent through confidence-aware box prompts, while semantic-dominated targets are handled by an MLLM-based Semantic Agent through description-guided localization and candidate verification. On the MOSEv2 test set, VOS-Agent achieves 69.82% on the official $\mathcal{J}\&\dot{\mathcal{F}}$ metric and ranks first in the MOSEv2 Track of the 8th LSVOS Challenge at ECCV 2026.

</details>


### [100] [A Generative Approach for Improving Multi-Label Defect Classification in Photovoltaic Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12725)
*Abdul Mueez,Yogesh S. Rawat,Shruti Vyas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出生成式缺陷隔离（GDI）方法，利用LaMa修补模型（含快速傅里叶卷积）从光伏电池电致发光（EL）图像中移除选定缺陷，生成逼真的单缺陷训练样本，以解决多缺陷共现导致的学习歧义和稀有缺陷类别样本不足问题。实验表明GDI在多种架构上显著超越基线，稀有缺陷F1分数最高提升63.6%，共现分类错误降低26%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多标签缺陷分类中，多个缺陷共现的图像会造成学习歧义，难以将视觉特征与具体缺陷类型对应；同时单个类别样本稀缺进一步加剧了训练难度。

Method: 引入生成式缺陷隔离（GDI），利用带有快速傅里叶卷积的LaMa修补模型，将选定缺陷从EL图像中移除，从而生成逼真的单缺陷训练样本，缓解多缺陷共现带来的歧义。

Result: 在ViT-S、ViT-L和EfficientNetV2-L架构上，GDI均显著优于基线。低数据场景下增益最为明显，稀有缺陷类别的F1分数最高提升63.6%，同时共现分类错误降低了26%。

Conclusion: GDI是最大化现有分割数据集价值的有效方法，为光伏电池EL图像多标签分类领域设立了新的性能基准。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of multi-label defect classification in electroluminescence (EL) images of photovoltaic (PV) cells. Training models on images where multiple defects co-occur creates learning ambiguity, making it difficult to disentangle visual features for specific defect types, a problem compounded by the scarcity of examples for individual classes. To tackle this, we introduce Generative Defect Isolation (GDI), utilizing the LaMa inpainting model with Fast Fourier Convolutions to remove selected defects and generate realistic, single-defect training samples. Extensive experiments on Vision Transformer (ViT-S, ViT-L) and EfficientNetV2-L architectures demonstrate that GDI significantly outperforms baselines. The performance gains are most pronounced in low-data scenarios; class-wise analysis shows substantial improvements, boosting the F1-Score for rare defect classes by up to 63.6%. Furthermore, GDI effectively resolves learning ambiguity from co-occurring defects, yielding a 26% reduction in such co-occurring classification errors. Our work establishes GDI as an effective method for maximizing the value of existing segmentation datasets and sets a new performance benchmark for multi-label classification in this domain.

</details>


### [101] [Dual-Manifold Geometry Guided Representation Learning: Adaptive Coupling between Kernel and Data Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12737)
*Wencong Zhang,Yue Zhang,Meiyan Huang,Wei Yang,Qianjin Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双流形视角下的核引导特征变换（KGFT）方法，通过将卷积核形成的核流形几何信息迁移到数据流形来改造特征表示的协方差结构，并引入深度感知的Exploit/Explore调度策略，在CNN和Transformer架构上均取得了稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度表示学习主要关注特征如何跨网络层演化，却极大忽视了网络参数中嵌入的结构化几何信息。作者认为卷积层包含由卷积滤波器诱导的核流形和由中间特征表征的数据流形，两者共享同一通道空间，因此参数几何可以为特征演化提供互补的结构性指导。

Method: 提出KGFT模块：从核Gram矩阵推导几何引导矩阵，用于变换特征表示的协方差结构。与仅重新加权特征响应的传统注意力机制不同，KGFT通过将几何信息从核流形传递到数据流形来显式重塑特征关系。引入Exploit和Explore两种模式，配合深度感知调度策略和可学习的引导强度，在浅层促进几何对齐、在深层鼓励特征多样性。

Result: 在CNN和Transformer架构（ResNet、ViT、LLaMA-7B）上针对图像分类和算术推理任务进行了广泛实验，均取得了一致改进，验证了核引导双流形表示学习的通用性和有效性。理论分析也确立了所提变换的有效性并刻画了其对特征协方差的影响。

Conclusion: 参数几何包含可用于指导特征表示学习的结构性信息；通过双流形视角和KGFT模块，可以更充分地利用核流形到数据流形的几何传递来增强表示学习，且该方法跨架构、跨任务均具有泛化能力。

Abstract: Deep representation learning has primarily focused on how features evolve across network layers, while largely overlooking the structured geometry embedded in network parameters. We introduce a dual-manifold perspective in which each convolutional layer contains two coupled geometric spaces: a Kernel Manifold induced by convolutional filters and a Data Manifold characterized by intermediate feature representations. Because these manifolds share the same channel space, parameter geometry can provide complementary structural information to guide feature evolution. Based on this insight, we propose Kernel-Guided Feature Transform (KGFT), a lightweight module that derives a geometric guidance matrix from the kernel Gram matrix and uses it to transform the covariance structure of feature representations. Unlike conventional attention mechanisms that reweight feature responses, KGFT explicitly reshapes feature relationships by transferring geometric information from the kernel manifold to the data manifold. To accommodate network hierarchy, we further introduce Exploit and Explore modes with a depth-aware scheduling strategy and a learnable guidance strength that adaptively controls the contribution of geometric transformation. This design promotes geometric alignment in shallow layers while encouraging feature diversity in deeper layers, without imposing excessive constraints on representation learning. Theoretical analysis establishes the validity of the proposed transformation and characterizes its effect on feature covariance. Extensive experiments across CNN- and Transformer-based architectures, including ResNet, ViT, and LLaMA-7B, demonstrate consistent improvements on image classification and arithmetic reasoning tasks, validating the generality and effectiveness of kernel-guided dual-manifold representation learning. Code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [102] [Dual-Stream Cross-Anchor Correction Grounding Long-Form Captions and the Domain Limits of Object-Level Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12746)
*LingKai Bu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DSCC（Dual-Stream Cross-Anchor Correction），首次在微调阶段将对象级视觉锚点注入多模态大语言模型，通过感知流（双向对比学习对齐对象级隐状态与冻结文本锚点）和认知流（交叉注意力在每步生成时查询锚点）以及两阶段课程门控，将证据检索变成自回归生成的结构性约束，在长描述幻觉和物体存在判别任务上显著抑制幻觉并保持长描述能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型中的对象幻觉源于语言先验和语料共现偏差压过视觉证据，导致生成的描述与图像内容脱节。现有方法大多在解码时干预（无训练），但仅对短描述有效；监督微调虽能加长描述，却仍有超过40%的描述提及不存在的物体。因此需要在模型结构中显式注入视觉证据约束，而非仅做后处理。

Method: DSCC在微调阶段向语言模型注入对象级视觉锚点：1）感知流利用双向对比目标将中间层的对象级隐状态与冻结文本锚点对齐；2）认知流让更深层在每步生成时通过交叉注意力查询这些锚点；3）两阶段课程门控耦合两条流，使证据检索成为每个自回归步骤的结构性约束。

Result: 在统一骨干网络和评分协议下，DSCC是唯一同时达到长描述和低幻觉区域的方法：描述长度约为基线1.9倍，每个对象提及的精度达88.19%，为密度无关标准下的最高值。消融实验揭示协同效应：感知流单独会降低精度，但叠加在认知流上时效果反转。三个域外基准显示可预测的域条件性，协同效应局限于锚点语义域，在图表和视错觉上失效。

Conclusion: 将对象级视觉锚点注入语言模型微调的结构性约束相比解码时干预和普通SFT更有效，但该方法不具备普适优越性——其效果与锚点的语义域绑定，在图表和视错觉等非自然图像场景中失效，体现了可证伪的域条件性。

Abstract: Object hallucination in multimodal large language models arises when language priors and corpus co-occurrence bias outweigh the visual evidence, with nothing tying an individual object mention to what the image shows. Most remedies intervene at decoding time without training, yet under a unified protocol their benefit is confined to short captions;supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on a detail- rich corpus lengthens captions, but over forty percent still name absent objects. This paper proposes Dual-Stream Cross-Anchor Correction (DSCC). Unlike work that post-processes decoding, DSCC is the first to inject object-level visual anchors into the language model itself during fine- tuning: a perception stream aligns object-level hidden states at an intermediate layer to frozen text anchors by a bidirectional contrastive objective; a cognition stream lets deeper layers query those anchors by cross-attention at every generation step; and a two-stage curriculum gate couplesthem, making evidence retrieval a structural constraint at each autoregressive step. Under one backbone and one scoring protocol, experiments span long-caption hallucination, object-existence discrimination and cross-domain generalisation, with vanilla SFT on the same corpus and schedule as a length- and density-matched control, so gains are attributed layer by layer. DSCC is the only method reaching the long-caption, low-hallucination region: captions roughly 1.9 times the baseline length at 88.19% precision per object mention, the highest under a density-independent criterion. Ablations expose a synergy: the perception stream alone degrades precision yet reverses sign when stacked on the cognition stream. No universal superiority is claimed: three out-of- domain benchmarks yield a predictable, falsifiable domain-conditionality, the synergy being bound to the anchors' semantic domain and breaking on charts and optical illusions.

</details>


### [103] [Scaling Representation Diversity: Modulated Attention and Reconstructive Regularization for Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12748)
*Junyi Hu,Tian Bai,Fengyi Wu,Yian Huang,Wei Wen,Zaoli Li,Junli Lin,Xingchen Li,Zhenming Peng,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据-模型协同设计的统一开放词汇指代表达理解（REC）框架。通过引入调制注意力对比头（mACH）和文本条件JEPA辅助流来保留表示多样性，并构建Objects365-Caption数据集提供大规模语言监督，实现单一通用模型在多个异构grounding数据集上的强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有REC方法通常针对特定数据集进行微调，产生仅适用于单一数据的专用模型，跨数据集泛化能力有限。作者识别出表示退化（representation degeneration）是阻碍训练单一通用模型的关键障碍，因此希望从统一开放词汇grounding的角度重新审视REC问题。

Method: 提出数据-模型协同设计框架：（1）架构上引入调制注意力对比头mACH实现高效的token级视觉-语言对齐；（2）设计文本条件JEPA辅助流提供互补梯度支持，在不增加推理开销的情况下保留对齐活跃的表示；（3）数据方面构建Objects365-Caption数据集，为Objects365添加上下文感知的指代表达以提供大规模语言监督。同时提供理论分析证明互补梯度子空间能保持对齐能力并扩展表示多样性。

Result: 大量实验表明，该单检查点框架在标准REC基准上取得有竞争力的性能，同时在异构grounding数据集上展现出无需基准特定适配的强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过架构与数据两方面的协同创新，成功解决了统一REC模型中表示退化的问题，证明单一通用模型可以在不牺牲标准基准性能的前提下实现跨数据集泛化，为开放词汇REC研究提供了一种新范式。

Abstract: Referring Expression Comprehension (REC) is commonly studied under dataset-specific fine-tuning, resulting in specialist models with limited cross-dataset generalization. In this work, we revisit REC from the perspective of unified open-vocabulary grounding and identify representation degeneration as a key obstacle to scaling a single generalist model. To preserve representation diversity, we propose a holistic data-model co-design framework. Architecturally, we introduce the Modulated Attention-Contrastive Head (mACH) for efficient token-level vision-language alignment and a text-conditioned JEPA auxiliary stream that provides complementary gradient support to preserve alignment-active representations without inference overhead. On the data side, we introduce Objects365-Caption, enriching Objects365 with context-aware referring expressions for large-scale language supervision. We further provide a theoretical analysis showing that complementary gradient subspaces preserve alignment capacity and thereby scale representation diversity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our single-checkpoint framework achieves highly competitive performance on standard REC benchmarks while exhibiting strong generalization across heterogeneous grounding datasets without benchmark-specific adaptation.

</details>


### [104] [PatchGen: Learning Soft Intra-Image Predictive Subsets for Visual Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12766)
*Zhaorui Tan,Weimiao Yu,Xi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出PatchGen，一种无需文本监督的模块，通过学习样本自适应的软预测子集掩码来限制预测于图像中的"oracle"子集，从而在不依赖领域不变性的前提下同时应对数据偏移、目标偏移及其组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉分类器应对分布偏移的方法多聚焦于域不变性，忽略了图像内部的预测充分性结构。作者假设每张图像中存在一个样本自适应的oracle子集足以进行标签预测，其余patch构成可能带有标签相关性的非必要互补上下文。

Method: 首先从理论上证明将预测限制于oracle子集可保持全patch表示的Bayes风险，同时提供随oracle子集大小收紧的复杂度界。在此基础上提出PatchGen，一种无需文本的模块，学习样本相关的软预测子集掩码，作为未观测oracle掩码的任务驱动代理。

Result: 在自然图像和病理图像基准上覆盖全部三种偏移设置（数据偏移、目标偏移及其组合），PatchGen在大多数配置下优于匹配骨干的基线，增强了对未知类的泛化，且无需文本监督即可与视觉-语言方法竞争。病理可视化显示PatchGen对肿瘤一致区域赋予比常见共现炎症上下文更高的分数。

Conclusion: PatchGen验证了"图像内oracle预测子集"这一结构性假设的有效性，提供了一条不依赖域不变性也不依赖文本监督的通用泛化改进路径，在多种偏移场景下提升分类器性能。

Abstract: Visual classifiers are expected to generalize under data shifts, target shifts, and their combinations, yet most existing methods focus on domain invariance while failing to address intra-image predictive sufficiency. We investigate the structural hypothesis that each image contains a sample-adaptive oracle intra-image predictive subset sufficient for label prediction, while the remaining patches form non-essential complementary context that may correlate with the label. The theoretical analysis shows that restricting prediction to this oracle subset preserves the Bayes risk achievable by the full-patch representation while admitting a complexity bound that tightens with the oracle-subset size. Based on this view, we propose PatchGen, a text-free module that learns a sample-dependent soft predictive-subset mask as a task-driven proxy for the unobserved oracle subset mask. Specifically, histopathology visualizations suggest that PatchGen assigns higher scores to tumor-consistent regions than to some frequently co-occurring inflammatory context. Extensive experiments on natural and histopathological image benchmarks spanning all three shift settings show that PatchGen improves average performance over matched-backbone baselines in most evaluated configurations, enhances generalization to unknown classes, and remains competitive with vision-language methods without text supervision.

</details>


### [105] [CW-BASS v2: Saturation-Aware Pseudo-Label Selection for Semi-Supervised Segmentation under Foundation-Model Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12773)
*Ebenezer Tarubinga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CW-BASS v2，一种针对自监督基础编码器（如DINOv2）下置信度饱和问题的伪标签选择方法。它通过配对保留集校准无偏估计每类噪声，并结合自适应置信度下限与严格过滤规则，以"one-pass"门控机制根据教师置信度状态自适应决定过滤策略，在多个饱和基准（Pascal VOC、Cityscapes）上恢复了UniMatch V2的操作点，并在不可靠置信集（ADE20K）上进一步改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统半监督语义分割中，伪标签选择依赖动态阈值、逐类课程、软置信权重等规则，这些是为噪声大、欠置信的ResNet教师设计的。自监督基础编码器（如DINOv2）作为教师时置信度饱和，传统过滤规则反而会损害强教师的表现。因此需要一个能识别教师置信度状态并自适应选择过滤策略的方法。

Method: CW-BASS v2包含三个关键组件：(1) 配对保留集校准——对每个类别进行无偏噪声估计；(2) 自适应置信度下限——可证明地将保留率约束在1以下；(3) one-pass门控——在保留集上测量教师置信集的可靠性（pi_kept = Pr[correct | c >= tau]），当满足所需置信度（pi_kept >= tau）时严格过滤，否则回退到自适应下限。该边界基于已有的操作阈值而非针对mIoU调参。

Result: 在六个DINOv2教师上，CW-BASS v2能盲判正确选择严格过滤还是下限策略。在饱和基准上恢复UniMatch V2操作点：Pascal VOC 1/8达87.4（报告87.9），Cityscapes差距在0.5以内；在置信集不可靠的ADE20K上（pi_kept约89%），下限策略领先+1.5 mIoU（单种子）。

Conclusion: CW-BASS v2证明在自监督基础编码器时代，伪标签选择的正确方式不是预设某一种规则，而是测量并响应教师当前的置信度状态。所避免的失败是可测量的而非假设的：在可靠且饱和的教师上，置信度动态范围崩塌（98%的Pascal像素>=0.95），自适应截止会淹没保留掩码，使自训练退化为确认偏差。

Abstract: Semi-supervised semantic segmentation has long turned on one question, which pseudo-labels to trust, and a generation of selection rules, dynamic thresholds, per-class curricula, soft confidence weights, answered it for the noisy, under-confident ResNet teachers of their day. Self-supervised foundation encoders change the regime: with a DINOv2 teacher, confidence saturates, so the filtering that helped a weak teacher can hurt a strong one. We propose CW-BASS v2, a saturation-aware pseudo-label selection method that reads the teacher's confidence regime rather than committing to one rule. It pairs held-out calibration, an unbiased per-class noise estimate, with a self-adaptive confidence floor that provably bounds retention away from 1, and combines them in a one-pass gate: measure the reliability of the teacher's confident set, pi_kept = Pr[correct | c >= tau], on a held-out slice, and filter strictly when it meets the confidence demanded (pi_kept >= tau), falling back to the adaptive floor otherwise. The boundary is the pre-existing operating threshold, not a value tuned to mIoU, and across six DINOv2 teachers it makes the correct strict-vs-floor call blind. CW-BASS v2 thus recovers the UniMatch V2 operating point on the saturated benchmarks by selecting strict (Pascal VOC 1/8 87.4 against its reported 87.9; Cityscapes within 0.5), and improves on it where the confident set is unreliable (pi_kept ~ 89%, ADE20K), where the floor edges ahead (+1.5 mIoU, single seed). The gate is principled because the failure it avoids is measured, not assumed: on a reliable, saturated teacher the confidence distribution's dynamic range collapses (98% of Pascal pixels >= 0.95), so an adaptive cutoff floods the retention mask and self-training decays into confirmation bias.

</details>


### [106] [SCOPE: Subspace Clustering with Online Per-Head Top-K Estimation for Sparse Video Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12780)
*Qi Zhao,Qirui Li,Hanlin Tang,Yiduo Li,Zhen Guo,Cuifeng Shen,Chao Xu,Zhaosheng Chi,Xiaojin Lu,Kan Liu,Tao Lan,Lin Qu,Xi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCOPE是一个训练无关的稀疏注意力框架，通过3D-RoPE对齐的键子空间聚类和在线逐头Top-k估计，在视频生成DiT模型中实现高效的稀疏注意力，相比现有方法在保真度和延迟上均有提升，最高可获得1.99倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练无关的稀疏注意力方法存在两个关键缺陷：(1) 基于块级或聚类级代理分数构建稀疏掩码会模糊键间的细粒度差异，在激进稀疏度下可能遗漏高贡献键；(2) 代理分数可能导致过度集中的softmax分布，使Top-p在部分查询簇中保留过少的键，而固定的Top-k最小值无法适应不同头和输入间的变化。

Method: SCOPE框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 3D-RoPE对齐的键子空间聚类——将post-RoPE键划分为时间、高度和宽度三个子空间分别聚类，通过查找表聚合相应的质心分数，为每个查询簇获得每个键的代理分数；(2) 在线逐头Top-k估计——在现有的混合Top-p/固定Top-k选择基础上，通过平均每个头内初始保留键数（按查询簇大小加权）推导出头特定的Top-k值，仅对初始保留键数低于该值的查询簇补充选择额外键。在所选原始键和值上计算稀疏注意力。

Result: 在六个模型-任务配置上，SCOPE在保真度和延迟两方面均一致优于现有的训练无关基线。在720p HunyuanVideo上，相对于密集注意力实现了最高1.99倍端到端加速，同时保持28.46 dB的PSNR。

Conclusion: SCOPE通过3D-RoPE对齐的子空间聚类和自适应逐头Top-k在线估计，有效解决了现有稀疏注意力方法中代理分数掩盖细粒度差异和固定阈值不适应头间/输入间变化的问题，为视频DiT推理提供了更优的训练无关稀疏注意力方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) incur quadratic self-attention cost over spatiotemporal tokens. Existing training-free sparse attention methods often construct sparse masks from block-level or cluster-level proxy scores, which can obscure fine-grained differences among keys and miss high contribution keys under aggressive sparsity. Moreover, such proxy scores may yield overly concentrated softmax distributions, causing Top-$p$ to retain too few keys for some query clusters. Although a fixed Top-$k$ minimum alleviates this failure mode, a shared value cannot adapt to variations across heads and inputs. To address both limitations, we propose SCOPE, a training-free sparse attention framework that combines 3D-RoPE-aligned key subspace clustering with online per-head Top-$k$ estimation for efficient video-DiT inference. SCOPE partitions post-RoPE keys into temporal, height, and width subspaces, clusters them independently, and aggregates the corresponding centroid scores through lookup tables to obtain per key proxy scores for each query cluster. Building on existing hybrid Top-$p$/fixed Top-$k$ selection, SCOPE derives a head-specific Top-$k$ value online by averaging the initial retained key counts within each head, weighted by query cluster size, and selects additional keys only for query clusters whose initial retained key counts fall below this value. Sparse attention is then computed over the selected original keys and values. Across six model--task configurations, SCOPE consistently outperforms existing training-free baselines in both fidelity and latency, achieving up to a $1.99\times$ end-to-end speedup on 720p HunyuanVideo with $28.46$ dB PSNR relative to dense attention.

</details>


### [107] [Beyond Correctness: Benchmarking and Aligning Response Behaviors in Hybrid-Thinking MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12781)
*Xinming Wang,Weinong Wang,Hongming Yang,Yansong Lin,Zheng Ruan,Shangpin Peng,Qiming Peng,Nan Qiao,Fengyuan Lu,Guoqing Ma,Marito Li,Songyang Zhang,Saiyong Yang,Han Hu,Yonglong Tian,Xu-Yao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PatternEval诊断基准和PatternRL训练框架，用于解决混合思维多模态大语言模型（MLLM）中思考模式与非思考模式之间的响应模式不对齐问题。PatternEval包含2,415个多模态提示，覆盖四类反复出现的响应模式失败（思维链泄漏、响应重复、逻辑矛盾、表演性推理）；PatternRL通过模式特定惩罚的强化学习来缓解跨模式对齐问题。在Qwen3-VL-4B和Qwen3-VL-8B上的实验表明，该方法能有效缓解跨模式不对齐，同时仅带来轻微的任务性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合思维MLLM允许在同一模型内交替进行深思熟虑的思考推理和低延迟的非思考推理，但两种模式虽然在推理预算上不同，其输出应满足相同的用户可见质量标准。仅靠正确性不足以刻画响应质量，且非思考推理表现出显著更高的响应模式失败率，造成系统性跨模式不对齐，因此需要专门的研究来诊断和缓解这一问题。

Method: 1) 构建PatternEval：包含2,415个多模态提示的失败增强诊断基准，覆盖视觉感知与接地、结构化图像理解、多模态知识推理三个领域，测试思维链泄漏、响应重复、逻辑矛盾、表演性推理四类失败模式。2) 提出PatternRM：响应级奖励模型，用于评估响应模式质量。3) 提出PatternRL：在强化学习过程中引入模式特定惩罚，以对齐思考与非思考接口的响应行为。

Result: 响应模式失败在来自不同提供商的多种模型中普遍存在；非思考推理的失败率显著更高，导致思考与非思考接口之间出现系统性不对齐。在Qwen3-VL-4B和Qwen3-VL-8B上的实验表明，PatternRL能在缓解跨模式不对齐的同时，仅带来边际的任务性能折损。

Conclusion: PatternEval和PatternRL共同构成了一套评估与训练框架，用于对齐混合思维接口中用户可见的响应模式，弥补了仅以正确性作为质量指标的研究空白。

Abstract: Hybrid-thinking multimodal large language models (MLLMs) allow a single model to alternate between deliberative thinking and latency-efficient non-thinking inference. Although these modes differ in reasoning budget, their delivered responses should satisfy the same user-facing standard. Correctness alone may not characterize this response quality; we therefore evaluate task accuracy and response-pattern failures as complementary outcomes. We study this gap through \textbf{response-pattern alignment}: whether thinking and non-thinking interfaces preserve acceptable final-response behavior. We introduce \textbf{PatternEval}, a failure-enriched diagnostic benchmark comprising 2,415 multimodal prompts spanning visual perception and grounding, structured image understanding, and multimodal knowledge reasoning. PatternEval tests four recurrent failures: chain-of-thought leakage, response repetition, logical contradiction, and performative reasoning. Response-pattern failures are widespread across models from different providers, with non-thinking inference exhibiting substantially higher failure rates and thereby creating systematic misalignment between thinking and non-thinking interfaces. Motivated by this diagnosis, we develop \textbf{PatternRM}, a response-level reward model, and \textbf{PatternRL}, which introduces pattern-specific penalties during reinforcement learning. Experiments on Qwen3-VL-4B and Qwen3-VL-8B show that incorporating pattern-specific penalties into reinforcement learning can mitigate cross-mode misalignment while incurring a marginal task performance trade-off. Together, PatternEval and PatternRL provide an evaluation-and-training framework for aligning user-visible response patterns across hybrid-thinking interfaces.

</details>


### [108] [Erase but Preserve: Controllable Removal of Copyrighted Animation Characters via Optimized Semantic Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12806)
*Qiao Li,Xiaomeng Fu,Wangjia Yu,Runze He,Baisen Wang,Jiao Dai,Jizhong Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对文生图扩散模型在动画角色生成中的版权问题，提出了一种基于连续文本表示的可控擦除方法，通过优化锚点嵌入作为角色替身，并采用结构感知自适应策略替换目标相关嵌入，实现高效的角色擦除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生图扩散模型的强大生成能力引发了版权担忧，尤其是动画角色的未授权复制。现有概念擦除方法在动画角色擦除上表现不佳：模型修改方法难以针对多样化角色找到合适锚点，提示词引导方法缺乏细粒度控制，两者均导致擦除不彻底或图像保真度下降。

Method: 在模型的连续文本表示上进行操作：首先通过结构性和细节性约束优化一个锚点嵌入作为目标角色的替身，然后采用结构感知的自适应策略将目标相关嵌入替换为锚点嵌入，实现生成过程中的可控擦除。

Result: 实验表明该方法在擦除效果和图像保真度保持上达到最先进水平，同时支持可控擦除程度、多目标移除和模型迁移性；优化得到的锚点还可即插即用地集成到现有模型修改基线中以提升其擦除性能。

Conclusion: 该工作通过在连续文本表征空间中进行可控操作，有效解决了动画角色擦除中锚点难找和细粒度控制不足的问题，为版权保护提供了实用且可扩展的技术方案。

Abstract: The exceptional generation capabilities of text-to-image diffusion models have raised copyright concerns, particularly the unauthorized reproduction of animation characters. Existing concept erasure methods fall short for animation character erasure: model modification methods struggle to identify suitable anchors for diverse, highly distinctive characters; prompt-based steering methods lack fine-grained control for precise intervention. These approaches often yield incomplete erasure and degraded image fidelity, hindering real-world deployment. In this paper, we propose a controllable method operating on the model's continuous textual representation to erase target characters during generation. We optimizes an anchor embedding via structural and detailed constraints to serve as a character surrogate, then replaces target-related embeddings with the anchor via a structure-aware adaptive strategy. Experiments show that our method achieves state-of-the-art erasure effectiveness and image fidelity preservation, while supporting controllable erasure degree, multi-target removal, and model transferability. Moreover, our optimized anchors are plug-and-play with current model modification baselines to improve their erasure performance.

</details>


### [109] [Structured Local Differential Modeling for AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12811)
*Jiazhen Yang,Ruijin Jin,Junjun Zheng,Xiangheng Kong,Zunlei Feng,Jie Lei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RippleNet是一个基于局部差分信号的AI生成图像检测框架，通过缓解语义成分的支配、放大低信噪比伪造痕迹，并在局部差分表示空间内细化注意力机制，从而在多个公开基准和跨生成器评估中取得竞争性的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测方法在训练过程中倾向于关注信噪比高的语义显著成分，从而抑制了与生成机制相关的、嵌入在低层统计结构中的细微取证线索（低信噪比伪造痕迹），导致检测性能受限。

Method: 从信息论视角出发，提出RippleNet框架：1) 通过局部差分信号自适应识别伪造敏感区域，在局部邻域内构建多方向、多尺度的差分表示，显式刻画邻域统计中的异常模式；2) 将注意力机制细化到局部差分表示空间中，在更细的统计粒度上建立显式依赖关系，从而捕获传统卷积或全局图像级patch注意力难以建模的像素级伪造痕迹。

Result: 在多个公开基准数据集以及跨生成器评估设置下，RippleNet均取得了一致且具有竞争力的检测性能。

Conclusion: RippleNet通过强调低信噪比伪造痕迹并细化到局部差分统计空间中的注意力机制，有效提升了AI生成图像的检测能力，验证了在低层统计空间中缓解语义成分支配对检测有效性的关键作用。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of AI-generated content has made the reliable detection of generated images an increasingly critical challenge. Existing detection methods are often dominated during training by semantically salient components with high signal-to-noise ratios (SNRs), thereby suppressing subtler forensic cues associated with the underlying generation mechanisms and embedded in low-level statistical structures. From an information-theoretic perspective, we present a key insight: effective detection in the low-level statistical space requires mitigating the dominance of semantic components while emphasizing and amplifying responses to low-SNR forgery traces. Building on this insight, we propose RippleNet, an AI-generated image detection framework based on local differential signals. RippleNet adaptively identifies forgery-sensitive regions and constructs multi-directional, multi-scale differential representations within local neighborhoods, explicitly characterizing anomalous patterns in neighborhood statistics. More importantly, we refine the attention mechanism to operate within the local differential representation space, enabling the model to establish explicit dependencies at a finer statistical granularity. This design facilitates the capture of pixel-level forgery traces that are difficult to model using conventional convolutions or image-wide patch-level attention. Extensive experiments on multiple public benchmarks and under cross-generator evaluation settings demonstrate that RippleNet achieves consistently competitive performance.

</details>


### [110] [LocusGS: Spatially Grounded Tokens for Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12825)
*Wenyu Li,Sidun Liu,Tongrui Hu,Peng Qiao,Yong Dou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LocusGS为每个查询式3DGS的query增加显式3D锚点状态（中心和支撑半径），通过渐进精炼、锚点-射线几何偏置及锚点中心解码，显著提升查询级空间连贯性，在相同高斯预算下提高渲染质量并生成更结构化的高斯分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于查询的前馈3DGS方法中，每个可学习查询解码出的高斯常常散落在场景中相距较远的区域，导致查询级空间连贯性弱，与场景结构对齐差。作者认为这是由查询的纯隐式表征所致。

Method: LocusGS为每个高斯查询增加一个3D锚点状态，包含中心（center）和支撑半径（support radius）。锚点状态在解码器各层间渐进精炼，并贯穿查询交互、多视图特征聚合和高斯生成全过程。具体包含：1) 锚点-射线几何偏置，引导查询关注空间相关的图像观测；2) 锚点中心解码，将生成的高斯限制在局部区域内。

Result: 在新视图合成基准上，LocusGS在相同高斯预算下优于基于查询的高斯token基线方法；进一步分析表明，学习到的锚点形成连贯的空间布局，高斯分布更有结构。

Conclusion: 显式的锚点状态能够有效改善查询式3DGS的空间组织，验证了将几何显式先验引入查询表征的必要性和有效性。

Abstract: Recent query-based feed-forward 3DGS methods represent a scene using learnable queries, each aggregating multi-view evidence and decoding a group of Gaussians. Ideally, different queries should specialize in coherent local regions of the scene. However, we observe that Gaussians decoded from the same query often scatter across distant scene regions, resulting in weak query-level spatial coherence and poor alignment with the scene structure. We attribute this behavior to the purely latent representation of existing Gaussian queries. To address this limitation, we introduce LocusGS, which augments each Gaussian query with a 3D anchor state consisting of a center and a support radius. The anchor state is progressively refined across decoder layers and is used throughout query interaction, multi-view feature aggregation, and Gaussian generation. Specifically, an anchor-to-ray geometric bias guides each query toward spatially relevant image observations, while anchor-centered decoding organizes its Gaussians within a local region. Experiments on novel view synthesis benchmarks show that LocusGS improves rendering quality over query-based Gaussian token baselines under the same Gaussian budget. Further analysis shows that the learned anchors form coherent spatial layouts and lead to more structured Gaussian distributions, demonstrating that explicit anchor states improve the spatial organization. Our project page: https://leo-frank.github.io/LocusGS_viewer.

</details>


### [111] [Validation of Smartphone-Based Photogrammetric 3D Body Scanning for Automated Anthropometric Measurements Compared with a Commercial Depth-Sensor-Based Body Scanner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12827)
*Ruting Cheng,Boyuan Feng,Chuhui Qiu,Joaquin A. Calderon,Qing Pan,Yufan Liu,James K. Hahn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究系统验证了基于智能手机摄影测量的3D全身扫描应用（PolyCam）在全身测量提取和纵向体型监测中的有效性。以144名孕妇在孕期全程的纵向扫描数据为对象，将PolyCam与商用深度传感器设备Fit3D ProScanner进行对比，结果显示两者具有高度一致性（平均偏差低于16 mm，ICC>0.8，Pearson r>0.9），且刚性模特实验显示PolyCam与卷尺测量无显著差异（平均偏差<3.5 mm）。研究支持智能手机摄影测量作为商用全身扫描仪的可行替代方案，适用于纵向3D体型评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然智能手机摄影测量重建技术提供了一种低成本、易获取的3D全身扫描替代方案，但其在全身扫描场景中的性能尚未得到充分验证，尤其在医疗健康应用中需要严格的精度和一致性评估。

Method: 使用144名孕期纵向扫描的受试者，以商用深度传感器设备Fit3D ProScanner为基准，评估代表性摄影测量应用PolyCam。设计了自动周长提取管线，在4个解剖标志点提取测量值；采用线性混合效应模型评估扫描仪效应和纵向体型变化；通过重复PolyCam扫描和刚性模特上的卷尺测量评估一致性。

Result: PolyCam与Fit3D具有强一致性：所有标志点平均偏差低于16 mm，ICC高于0.8，Pearson相关系数高于0.9；两种系统捕捉到可比的纵向体型变化；模特实验中PolyCam平均偏差低于3.5 mm，与卷尺测量无显著差异。

Conclusion: 智能手机摄影测量可作为商用身体扫描仪的潜在可获取替代方案，并适用于纵向3D体型评估，为孕期等医疗场景中的低成本体型监测提供了实证支持。

Abstract: 3D body scanning has become an important tool in healthcare applications because of its rapid and non-invasive nature. While smartphone-based photogrammetric reconstruction provide a low-cost and accessible alternative to commercial 3D body scanners, their performance for whole-body scanning remains insufficiently validated. Thus, we designed this study to comprehensively validate the photogrammetric 3D scanning application by evaluating automatically extracted whole-body measurements and longitudinal body-shape monitoring. We evaluated a representative application, PolyCam, against the commercial depth-sensor-based Fit3D ProScanner using 144 pregnant participants scanned longitudinally throughout pregnancy. We designed an automatic circumference extraction pipeline to get measurements at four anatomical landmarks from paired 3D scans. A linear mixed-effects model was used to evaluate scanner effects and longitudinal body-shape changes. Measurement consistency was assessed using repeated PolyCam scans and tape measurements on a rigid mannequin. PolyCam demonstrated strong agreement with Fit3D, with average biases below 16 mm, intraclass correlation coefficients above 0.8, and Pearson correlation coefficients above 0.9 across all landmarks. Both systems captured comparable longitudinal body-shape changes. Mannequin experiments showed mean biases below 3.5 mm and no significant differences from tape measurements. These findings support smartphone photogrammetry as a potential accessible alternative to commercial body scanners and applicable for longitudinal 3D body-shape assessment.

</details>


### [112] [Semantic Steering for Controllable Generation: Tuning-Free Concept Erasure in Multimodal Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12829)
*Qiao Li,Xiaomeng Fu,Yuanshu Zhao,Qipeng Wang,Jiao Dai,Jizhong Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练、不修改参数的概念擦除方法，通过从MM-DiT中间块的文本条件语义表示中构造steering vector并注入早期和中间块，实现对多模态扩散变换器生成内容的有效概念消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念擦除方法大多需要修改模型参数，且依赖特定架构，难以应用于大规模部署的MM-DiT模型；而无需调优的方法在面对MM-DiT时因知识嵌入深、语义空间广和上下文相关文本编码器而效果不佳。

Method: 首先分析MM-DiT各块的生成角色，发现文本条件语义表征在中间块最为显著；从中提取不想要概念与安全概念的表示差异构造steering vector；将该单一向量注入到连续的早期和中间块，仅操作稀疏的文本分支token，利用rectified flow的直线采样轨迹实现概念擦除。

Result: 在多种MM-DiT模型上实验表明，该方法在擦除多种概念上达到最优性能，能有效控制最终输出，并对对抗攻击具有鲁棒性，且开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 通过直接操纵模型内部表示而非模型参数，可实现高效、训练-free且架构通用的概念擦除，为大规模MM-DiT模型的安全控制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Diffusion Transformers (MM-DiTs) have demonstrated remarkable text-to-image generation performance, surpassing traditional U-Net-based diffusion models. Nevertheless, their powerful generative capabilities also raise significant safety concerns, as they may generate sensitive or inappropriate content. While existing concept erasure methods aim to mitigate such risks, most require modifying model parameters, which are often architecture-specific and impractical for deployed larger models. Several tuning-free approaches face challenges when applied to advanced large-scale MM-DiTs due to their deeply embedded knowledge, broad semantic space, and context-dependent text encoders. To address these challenges, we propose to erase concepts by directly manipulating the model's internal representations. Our key insight, derived from an in-depth analysis of MM-DiT's block-wise generative roles, is that text-conditioned semantic representations are most salient in the middle blocks of MM-DiTs. Based on this, we extract representations of an unwanted concept and a desirable safe one from the middle block, construct a steering vector from their difference, and inject this single vector into consecutive early and middle blocks. By operating exclusively on the sparse text-branch tokens and leveraging the straight sampling trajectory of rectified flow, our method achieves effective concept erasure with negligible overhead and without any training. Extensive experiments across MM-DiT models demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in erasing diverse concepts, enables effective control over the final output, and remains robust to adversarial attacks.

</details>


### [113] [Heterogeneous Vision-Language Ensemble with Disagreement-Aware Reranking for Text-Based Person Anomaly Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12843)
*Huu-An Vu,Cam Tu Tran Thi,Thanh Toan Le Ngo,Hoang Vo,Do Trung Hieu,Hieu Dinh Trung Pham,Khang Minh Le,Huy Minh Nhat Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GENAI4E团队在AI City Challenge 2026 Track 4的解决方案，针对基于文本的人物异常行为检索任务，通过强检索骨干网络、异构视觉-语言嵌入模型的分数对齐与迭代集成融合，以及分歧感知的VLM重排序，在官方PAB基准上达到90.92% mAP、85.13% Recall@1，验证了互补视觉语言表征与选择性多模态推理的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于文本的人物异常行为检索比传统文本人物检索更具挑战性，需要对人物外观、行为、物体交互和场景上下文进行细粒度推理，从而提升跨模态匹配的鲁棒性。

Method: 构建强检索骨干网络，通过分数对齐和迭代集成融合渐进整合多个异构视觉-语言嵌入模型，并对模糊查询采用分歧感知的VLM重排序策略。

Result: 在官方Pedestrian Anomaly Behavior基准上取得90.92% mAP、85.13% Recall@1、97.72% Recall@5和98.68% Recall@10的成绩。

Conclusion: 结合互补的视觉-语言表征与选择性多模态推理，能够有效应对大规模文本人物异常检索任务。

Abstract: Text-based person anomaly retrieval aims to retrieve pedestrians exhibiting anomalous behaviors from a large image gallery using natural language descriptions. Compared with conventional text-based person retrieval, this task requires fine-grained reasoning over pedestrian appearance, behaviors, object interactions, and scene context, making robust cross-modal matching significantly more challenging. This paper presents the GENAI4E team's solution to AI City Challenge 2026 Track 4. Our framework builds upon a strong retrieval backbone and progressively integrates heterogeneous vision-language embedding models through score alignment and iterative ensemble fusion, followed by disagreement-aware VLM reranking for ambiguous queries. On the official Pedestrian Anomaly Behavior (PAB) benchmark, our approach achieves 90.92% mAP, 85.13% Recall@1, 97.72% Recall@5, and 98.68% Recall@10, demonstrating the effectiveness of combining complementary vision-language representations with selective multimodal reasoning for large-scale text-based person anomaly retrieval.

</details>


### [114] [SPARED: Reasoning-Based AI-Generated Image Detection via Adversarially Edited Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12876)
*Yicheng Bao,Xiahui Guo,Xuhong Wang,Xin Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种对抗强化学习框架，通过扩散图像编辑器生成针对当前检测器的对抗样本，同时用推理多模态大模型提供自由形式的判定理由，使AI生成图像检测器在动态攻防中不断泛化并自动提升解释质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测器存在三个缺陷：正负样本来源不同导致捷径（provenance shortcuts）、监督式解释语料导致模板化理由、静态伪造语料使决策边界无法跟上不断演进的生成器。

Method: 构建两个异构模型对抗的强化学习框架：扩散图像编辑器学习将真实照片编辑成能欺骗当前检测器的伪造版本，推理多模态大模型（MLLM）学习基于自由形式推理给出判定；通过反捷径奖励设计（攻击者仅在编辑忠实执行时获得奖励，防御者仅在判定正确时获得奖励），交替训练使每个轮次生成更难的训练池，迫使检测器泛化而非记忆固定伪影分布。

Result: 检测器在三个外部基准上每个轮次单调提升；虽然解释从未被直接奖励，但其质量随轮次提高而自动提升（作为仅基于准确率训练的副产物）。

Conclusion: 对抗式动态训练循环不仅能持续提升检测性能，还能在无监督解释目标的情况下自然涌现高质量的自由形式判定理由，为AI生成图像检测器的部署可信度提供了解决方案。

Abstract: Detecting AI-generated images is only half the task: a deployed detector must also justify its verdict, yet existing detectors inherit three failure modes from their training data: real and fake images collected from different sources invite provenance shortcuts, supervised explanation corpora teach templated rationales, and a static forgery corpus leaves the decision boundary standing still while generators keep moving. We introduce \methodname{}, an adversarial reinforcement learning framework that pits two heterogeneous models against each other. A diffusion image editor learns to edit real photographs into fake counterparts of those same photographs that fool the current detector, while a reasoning MLLM learns to expose them with a verdict grounded in free-form reasoning. Both rewards are shortcut-proof by design: the attacker is credited only when its edit is faithfully executed, and the defender only when its verdict is correct. As the two models alternate, each round's attacker regenerates a harder training pool aimed at the current detector's blind spots, so the detector must generalize rather than memorize any fixed artifact distribution. Although the explanation is never rewarded, its quality rises round over round as a side effect of accuracy-only training. A detector trained within this loop improves monotonically across rounds on each of three external benchmarks.

</details>


### [115] [NaviDC-OCR: Navigating Document Parsing Across Digital and Camera-Captured Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12898)
*Peng Cai,Zhaofan Zou,Shifa Liu,Yikun Wang,Jiawei Tang,Kaicheng Yang,Meng Tong,Zhongjiang He,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NaviDC-OCR是一个统一的文档解析框架，通过变形感知学习、自适应采样机制和内容-结构解耦学习策略，解决了现有VLM方法在文档解析中因几何形变导致级联误差以及高分辨率场景下冗余生成、幻觉和结构推理不足的问题，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VLM的文档解析方法面临两大挑战：一是解耦式方法依赖精确的版面分析，相机拍摄文档的几何形变会引入级联误差；二是端到端方法虽减轻了对显式版面检测的依赖，但在高分辨率场景下存在冗余生成、幻觉和结构推理不足的问题。

Method: 提出NaviDC-OCR统一框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 变形感知学习（deformation-aware learning），将几何感知融入VLM；(2) 自适应采样机制（adaptive sampling），用于复杂版面表示；(3) 内容-结构解耦学习策略（content-structure decoupled learning），显式建模公式语法和表格结构，实现更有效的结构化表示学习。

Result: 在OmniDocBench v1.6、Wild-OmniDocBench和PureDocBench上分别获得96.87、88.53和78.41的总体分数，并在ICDAR 2026 Sci-ImageMiner Challenge中排名第一，验证了方法的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: NaviDC-OCR通过同时建模几何形变和显式结构推理，有效解决了现有VLM文档解析方法的两大核心挑战，在复杂文档解析场景中展现出卓越的性能和泛化能力。

Abstract: Document parsing aims to transform unstructured documents into structured and machine-readable representations. Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have significantly advanced document parsing. However, existing approaches still face two major challenges. First, decoupled VLM-based methods heavily rely on accurate layout analysis, where geometric distortions in camera-captured documents can introduce cascading errors. Second, although end-to-end VLM-based methods alleviate the dependence on explicit layout detection, they often suffer from redundant generation, hallucinations, and insufficient structural reasoning in high-resolution scenarios. To address these challenges, we propose NaviDC-OCR, a unified framework for document parsing. NaviDC-OCR introduces deformation-aware learning to incorporate geometric perception into VLMs and proposes an adaptive sampling mechanism for complex layout representation. Furthermore, a content-structure decoupled learning strategy is developed to explicitly model formula grammars and table structures, enabling more effective structured representation learning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that NaviDC-OCR achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse document parsing benchmarks. It obtains overall scores of 96.87, 88.53 and 78.41 on OmniDocBench v1.6, Wild-OmniDocBench, and PureDocBench, respectively, and ranks first in the ICDAR 2026 Sci-ImageMiner Challenge. These results validate the effectiveness and generalization capability of NaviDC-OCR in complex document parsing scenarios.

</details>


### [116] [HounsWorld: A Multimodal World Model for Hidden Patient-State Readout, Reconstruction, and Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12904)
*Yunhao Bai,Zhongwei Qiu,Guangyu Guo,Yiming Huang,Tony C. W. Mok,Qinji Yu,Ling Zhang,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CT中心患者状态推理框架，通过共享潜在患者状态的隐式估计来统一CT影像的读取（readout）、重建（reconstruction）和模拟（simulation）三类任务。作者构建了HounsBench基准数据集（患者不重叠划分，按任务族评估）和HounsWorld——一个3B参数的多模态世界模型，通过联合理解生成学习将体扫描和语言视为共享状态的观测。零初始化的CT适配器保留预训练多模态映射，条件显式Hounsfield单位窗口采样提供临床相关的密度观测，在三个任务族上均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床智能需要从不完整的观测中推断患者潜在状况，而非从扫描到答案的孤立映射学习。现有的方法将CT理解、报告生成和图像生成分别建模，忽略了它们本质上都是对共享患者状态的依赖预测问题。作者希望通过统一框架将这三个任务族整合，以更贴近临床推理本质。

Method: 提出了HounsWorld，一个3B参数的多模态世界模型，采用共享Transformer形成隐式患者状态估计。支持三类输出：查询条件回答（读取状态）、报告和字幕（用语言重构状态）、条件CT体积生成（低剂量去噪、虚拟对比增强、文本和掩码到体积生成）。关键技术包括：1）联合理解生成学习框架，将体积扫描和语言作为共享状态的观测；2）零初始化CT适配器保留预训练多模态映射；3）条件显式Hounsfield单位窗口采样以暴露临床相关密度信息。配合构建的HounsBench基准，采用患者不重叠划分和每任务族指标。

Result: HounsWorld在三个任务族（读取、重建、模拟）上均表现强劲，并且通过临床结构化补全一致地提升了CT理解能力。实验结果验证了共享潜在患者状态框架的有效性，以及零初始CT适配器和Hounsfield窗口采样等设计选择对性能的贡献。

Conclusion: 以患者状态为中心的推理框架（HounsWorld及HounsBench）为CT临床智能提供了一种统一范式，将读取、重建和模拟统一为状态依赖的预测问题，优于将各任务孤立处理的方法，同时利用临床结构化任务互补地提升CT理解性能。

Abstract: Clinical intelligence requires estimating a patient's underlying condition from incomplete observations rather than learning isolated mappings from scans to answers. Volumetric medical images provide dense observations of anatomy, attenuation, and lesions, whereas clinical language provides sparse but complementary semantic observations. We formulate CT-centered intelligence as inference over a shared latent patient state, under which readout, reconstruction, and simulation all become state-dependent prediction problems. To operationalize this view, we introduce HounsBench, a computed tomography (CT) centric patient-state benchmark that unifies these three task families with patient-disjoint splits and per-family metrics, and HounsWorld, a 3B multimodal world model that treats volumetric scans and language as observations of the shared state through Joint Understanding-Generation Learning. A shared transformer forms an implicit patient-state estimate and supports three outputs: query-conditioned answers that read out the state, reports and captions that reconstruct it in language, and condition-specific CT volumes for low-dose denoising, virtual contrast enhancement, and anatomy-constrained text-and-mask-to-volume generation. Zero-initialized CT adapters preserve pretrained multimodal mappings, while condition-explicit Hounsfield-unit window sampling exposes clinically meaningful density observations. HounsWorld shows strong performance across all three task families while consistently improving CT understanding through clinically structured completion. Our project is available at https://github.com/byhwhite/HounsWorld.git

</details>


### [117] [Beyond Visual Evidence: Revealing and Mitigating Relational Privacy Leakage in Document MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12911)
*Beining Xu,Hairui Wang,Jiaxin Wang,Changsheng Chen,Anirban Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文聚焦文档理解多模态大模型（MLLMs）在身份证件处理中的隐私泄露问题，提出动态关系遗忘框架（DRUF）和DocPrivacyBench基准，在视觉证据匮乏时抑制模型对敏感字段关联关系的记忆和推断泄露。实验表明DRUF相较最强基线在泄露抑制上提升4.8个百分点，同时保持文档信息抽取性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多模态大模型（MLLMs）的隐私风险已受到广泛关注，但领域特定MLLMs的独特漏洞尚未被充分探索。特别是在身份证件处理这一高敏感场景中，当输入图像缺乏足够视觉证据时，模型会依赖训练数据中记忆的字段关系来推断缺失内容，导致多个相关联的敏感个人信息字段被集体泄露。现有研究未系统解决这一特定于KIE任务的隐私威胁。

Method: 本文提出三方面贡献：（1）Dynamic Relational Unlearning Framework (DRUF)，包含关系解耦遗忘（RDU）模块和动态集合更新机制，用于抑制高风险的字段对泄露同时保留KIE性能；（2）DocPrivacyBench，一个系统性评估模型在缺失或最小视觉证据条件下隐私泄露倾向的新基准；（3）使用该基准对三个MLLMs和六种遗忘方法进行评估，衡量遗忘后的泄露抑制效果和实用性保持。

Result: 实验表明现有MLLMs在视觉证据稀缺时普遍存在隐私泄露，尤其在噪声更大的数据集上更为严重。DRUF相比最强基线将泄露抑制提升了4.8个百分点，在有效缓解隐私风险的同时保持了稳健的文档信息抽取性能。

Conclusion: 本文率先揭示了领域特定MLLMs（特别是文档理解模型）在KIE任务中基于记忆字段关联进行隐私推断的特有漏洞，并提出了有效的缓解框架（DRUF）和评估基准（DocPrivacyBench），为该领域的隐私安全研究提供了新的工具和方向。

Abstract: While the privacy risks of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have drawn significant attention, the unique vulnerabilities of domain-specific MLLMs remain largely underexplored. Focusing on document understanding MLLMs for identity document processing, this paper investigates the privacy issues inherent in Key Information Extraction (KIE) tasks. We reveal that when input images lack sufficient visual evidence, these models often rely on memorized field relations from training data to infer missing content, thereby leaking multiple correlated fields containing sensitive personal information. To mitigate this risk, we make three key contributions.First, we propose the Dynamic Relational Unlearning Framework (DRUF) which comprises a Relational Decoupling Unlearning (RDU) module and a dynamic set update mechanism. It suppresses the leakage of high-risk field pairs while preserving KIE performance.Second, we introduce DocPrivacyBench, a novel benchmark to systematically evaluate a model's susceptibility to privacy leakage under conditions of absent or minimal visual evidence.Third, we evaluate three MLLMs and six unlearning methods using this benchmark, assessing both post-unlearning leakage suppression and utility preservation.Our results demonstrate that existing MLLMs consistently exhibit privacy leakage when visual evidence is scarce, particularly on noisier datasets. In contrast, DRUF outperforms the strongest baseline by improving leakage suppression by 4.8 percentage points, effectively mitigating privacy risks while maintaining robust document information extraction performance.

</details>


### [118] [TennisVAR: A Stroke-Evidence-Grounded Multimodal Large Language Model for Tactical Reasoning in Tennis Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12920)
*Yifan Mei,Qingling Shi,Changli Wu,Jiayuan Rao,Jiayi Ji,Liujuan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出击球证据引导的战术推理任务，并构建大规模专家标注基准TRACE和证据感知的多模态大语言模型TennisVAR，实现网球视频从事件感知到战术理解的跨越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网球视频理解方法存在两方面局限：要么只感知单个击球而缺乏对战术依赖关系的建模，要么生成高层分析却未锚定到底层事件之上。体育视频理解正从事件识别向解释动作如何共同塑造比赛进程演进，亟需桥接感知与理解之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出击球证据引导的战术推理任务，要求模型同时预测开放式答案、分层战术标签、有序击球证据序列和关键动作，且每个证据击球锚定到拍球接触帧。构建TRACE基准（含11,189个回合视频、41,485个击球事件、25,429个战术单元、11,189个问答对），并基于此提出TennisVAR模型，采用"事件-关系-证据-战术"推理范式：事件解析模块将连续回合转换为显式击球事件序列，战术图引导的时间推理器联合建模回合进展和同球员决策依赖以识别问题相关证据和关键动作。

Result: TRACE基准覆盖事实感知、战术理解和决策推理三个层次，统一了细粒度击球属性、跨击球战术关系、分层战术标注和证据锚定的问答。TennisVAR作为证据感知的多模态大语言模型，实现了从连续视频到战术推理的完整链条。

Conclusion: 通过提出新任务、构建大规模基准并设计证据锚定的推理模型，本文弥合了网球视频理解中感知与理解之间的差距，为体育视频的深层战术推理研究提供了范式与数据支撑。

Abstract: Sports-video understanding is moving beyond event recognition toward explaining how actions collectively shape match progression, however, existing tennis-video methods either perceive individual strokes without modeling their tactical dependencies or generate high-level analyses without grounding them in the underlying events. To bridge this perception-to-understanding gap, we formulate stroke-evidence-grounded tactical reasoning, a new rally-level task that requires models to jointly predict an open-ended answer, a hierarchical tactic label, an ordered sequence of supporting strokes, and decisive key actions, with each evidence stroke anchored to its racket-ball contact frame. We further introduce TRACE (Tactical Reasoning with Action-Chain Evidence in Tennis), a large-scale expert-annotated benchmark containing 11,189 rally videos, 41,485 stroke events, 25,429 tactical units, and 11,189 question-answer pairs, which unifies fine-grained stroke attributes, cross-stroke tactical relations, hierarchical tactic annotations, and evidence-grounded questions across factual perception, tactical understanding, and decision reasoning. Building on TRACE, we propose TennisVAR (Tennis Video Action-chain Reasoner), an evidence-grounded multimodal large language model that follows an "event-relation-evidence-tactic" reasoning paradigm, where an Event Parsing Module converts continuous rallies into explicit stroke-event sequences while a Tactical Graph-Guided Temporal Reasoner jointly models rally progression and same-player decision dependencies to identify question-relevant evidence and decisive actions.

</details>


### [119] [A Deep RL based Framework for Targeted White Matter Tractography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12960)
*Ankita Joshi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文提出一个混合框架，将强化学习与监督学习（基于GPT的策略学习）相结合，用于纤维束成像中的束特异追踪。该框架不依赖真实纤维标签进行训练，且通过束特异化公式绕过了显式分割过程。论文包含两个贡献：一是混合学习策略精炼方法，二是数据驱动的多策略融合框架，在TractoInferno、HCP和ISMRM-2015等基准数据集上验证了其有效性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纤维束成像在神经影像中至关重要，但白质结构的固有复杂性使其容易产生假阳性，导致关键通路的错误表征。现有方法依赖ground-truth纤维和显式分割过程，限制了其鲁棒性和实用性。因此需要一种不依赖真实标注、无需显式分割且能提高准确性和鲁棒性的束特异纤维束成像框架。

Method: 提出混合框架，将强化学习与监督学习（GPT-based policy learning）相结合以精炼RL策略，专用于束特异纤维束成像。框架不依赖ground-truth纤维训练，束特异公式化避免显式分割。具体包括两个贡献：(1) 混合强化学习与监督学习的策略精炼方法；(2) 可扩展的多策略融合框架，利用多个RL策略的互补优势提升性能与鲁棒性。

Result: 在TractoInferno、HCP和ISMRM-2015等公开基准数据集上进行了广泛验证，证明框架能够跨数据源泛化，准确重建大脑白质纤维束，提升了纤维束成像的鲁棒性、可靠性和准确性，同时减少了对真实标注的依赖。

Conclusion: 该混合框架在束特异纤维束成像中取得了显著进展，通过减少对ground-truth标注的依赖、简化管线（无需显式分割）以及多策略融合机制，提升了纤维束成像的鲁棒性、可靠性和准确性，代表了纤维束成像领域的重大进步。

Abstract: Fiber tractography's ability to reconstruct the brain's structural pathways, has made it a crucial component of modern neuroimaging, enabling detailed, non-invasive mapping of structural connectivity and supporting a wide range of neurological research and clinical applications. However, despite its importance, tractography remains a challenging task due to the inherent complexity of white matter structure and its susceptibility to false positives, which can lead to the misrepresentation of critical pathways.
  To overcome these limitations, in this thesis, we propose a hybrid framework that integrates reinforcement learning with supervised learning for refining RL policies, specifically tailored for tract-specific tractography. Notably, our framework does not rely on ground-truth fibers for training. Moreover, the tract-specific formulation bypasses the need for an explicit segmentation process, simplifying the overall pipeline.
  Our work includes two main contributions, each building upon the previous. First, we introduce a hybrid approach that combines reinforcement learning with supervised learning (specifically, GPT-based policy learning) to refine policies in a tract-specific context. Second, we propose a scalable framework for data-driven multi-policy fusion, which leverages the complementary strengths of multiple RL policies to improve tractography performance and robustness. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework through extensive validation on benchmark public datasets including TractoInferno, HCP, and ISMRM-2015, highlighting its ability to generalize across data sources and accurately reconstruct brain white matter tracts. We believe that these contributions represent significant advancements in the field of tractography, improving robustness, reliability, and accuracy while reducing dependence on ground-truth annotations.

</details>


### [120] [Bias Mitigation in Face Recognition via Demographic-based Supervised Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12971)
*Yu Linghu,Salman Mohammad,Xinyi Zhang,Manuel Günther*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于人口的监督对比损失（DeSCon），用于人脸识别中的公平性改进，通过精心设计的批次组合和人口感知的样本对选择，在保持竞争力的验证性能的同时改善不同人口统计群体间的错误率差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸识别系统在不同性别、年龄或种族群体间表现出不同的错误率，存在人口统计偏见。训练数据的类别不平衡是原因之一，但仅对训练数据进行人工平衡并不能完全缓解问题。由于实际部署中人脸识别通常在极低的误匹配率工作点运行（即非匹配分数分布的尾部），需要针对尾部行为进行改进以实现公平性。

Method: 提出DeSCon（Demographic-based Supervised Contrastive loss）损失函数，其核心包括：(1) 设计特定的训练批次组成方式，(2) 采用人口感知的样本对选择策略，旨在改善非匹配分数分布尾部的行为，从而在低误匹配率的工作点提升公平性。

Result: 在人口统计标注数据集和标准验证基准上的实验评估表明，DeSCon能够在超越数据平衡策略的基础上进一步提升公平性，同时保持具有竞争力的验证性能。

Conclusion: DeSCon证明了通过聚焦于分数分布尾部的损失函数设计，可以在不牺牲验证精度的情况下有效提升人脸识别系统的公平性，并且此方法可以与数据平衡等现有策略结合使用，提供更全面的公平性改进方案。

Abstract: Face recognition systems have been shown to be biased toward certain demographic groups by exhibiting different error rates across gender, age, or ethnicity. Though the imbalance of the training data with respect to these demographics is one cause of this bias, training on artificially balanced groups does not completely mitigate the problem. For deployment, face recognition typically works at operating points allowing very low false match rates and, hence, on the tail of the non-match score distribution. While class balancing can improve the means of these distributions, the aim of our approach is to improve fairness by addressing the behavior in the tail. Particularly, we propose the Demographic-based Supervised Contrastive loss (DeSCon) for face recognition, which relies on a well-designed composition of training batches and demographic-aware pair selection. Our experimental evaluation on both demographically-labeled datasets and standard verification benchmarks shows that DeSCon can improve fairness beyond balancing training datasets while maintaining competitive verification performance. Source code is available upon request.

</details>


### [121] [DiCoR: Decoupled Referent Disambiguation and Contour Recalibration for Efficient Referring Remote Sensing Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12980)
*Ziyang Gao,Zhizhuo Jiang,Jingjing Chang,Yixin Yang,Yuwen Pan,Yong-Qiang Mao,Yu Liu,Hai-Bao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DiCoR是一个基于高效JFS流程的解耦指代消歧与轮廓重校准框架，通过消歧感知的定位引导策略来区分模糊候选目标，并利用轻量级轮廓重校准模块细化粗分割掩码，在三个基准数据集上取得最优分割精度，同时比代表性DPS方法快4.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RRSIS方法存在两类缺陷：JFS方法效率高但精度有限，因为指代目标定位与掩码分割在统一目标下优化；DPS方法分离定位与掩码生成但内存消耗和推理延迟高。需要一种既保持JFS效率又能解决指代歧义和掩码粗糙问题的方法。

Method: DiCoR由两个核心模块组成：(1) 消歧感知的定位引导策略，通过自适应语言线索对显著候选区域进行排序，并将定位先验注入融合特征；(2) 轻量级轮廓重校准模块，在局部轮廓监督下预测粗logits的残差修正，以有限的计算开销提升掩码质量。

Result: 在RefSegRS、RRSIS-D和RISBench三个基准数据集上均取得最优分割精度。在RefSegRS上，mIoU和gIoU分别提升5.28%和2.87%，同时比代表性DPS方法运行速度快4.7%，展示了优越的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: DiCoR弥合了JFS和DPS之间的差距，在保持JFS高效性的同时，通过解耦消歧和轮廓重校准实现了与DPS相当乃至更优的精度，验证了在高效流程上融入针对性优化模块的有效性。

Abstract: Referring remote sensing image segmentation (RRSIS) aims to delineate targets specified by natural language expressions in remote sensing imagery. Existing methods mainly follow joint fusion segmentation (JFS) or decoupled prompt segmentation (DPS). JFS is efficient but often suffers from limited accuracy because referent localization and mask delineation are optimized under a unified objective, whereas DPS separates localization from mask generation using spatial prompts and foundation segmenters at the cost of higher memory consumption and inference latency. To bridge this gap, we propose DiCoR, a decoupled referent disambiguation and contour recalibration framework built on an efficient JFS pipeline. DiCoR addresses two key challenges: distinguishing the correct referent from ambiguous candidates and refining coarse masks after localization. A disambiguation-aware localization guidance strategy ranks salient candidate regions with adaptive linguistic cues and injects the resulting localization prior into fused features. A lightweight contour recalibration module further predicts residual corrections to coarse logits under localized contour supervision, improving mask quality with limited computational overhead. Experiments on RefSegRS, RRSIS-D, and RISBench show that DiCoR achieves the best segmentation accuracy across all three benchmarks. On RefSegRS, it improves mIoU and gIoU by 5.28% and 2.87% over a competitive JFS method while running 4.7% faster than a representative DPS method, demonstrating a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off. Code is available at https://github.com/zyGao1126/DiCoR.

</details>


### [122] [PixSDS: Why Latent SDS Makes Noisy Pixels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12997)
*Vsevolod Skorokhodov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文识别出潜在分数蒸馏采样（latent SDS）的失败模式：VAE引发的像素漂移导致优化图像在编码器弱约束方向上累积可见伪影，并提出PixSDS——一种轻量级、VAE一致的梯度修复方法，通过解码SDS lookahead步骤获得干净的像素空间优化方向，在不重训练扩散模型、不更换渲染器或不修改SDS目标的前提下显著减少结构化伪影并保持语义内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在SDS在文本到3D生成中会产生结构化的颜色伪影和高频纹理噪声。作者发现根本原因在于VAE编码器对像素空间某些方向约束不足，导致图像在这些方向上漂移时潜在表征仍然干净，而图像本身积累可见伪影。这一诊断基于受控2D SDS实验、仅VAE优化和简化分析。

Method: PixSDS方法的核心是对潜在SDS的lookahead步骤进行解码，将解码后的图像作为像素空间优化的干净方向，以此减小图像在VAE不一致方向上的运动。该方法不涉及扩散模型重训练、渲染器更换或SDS目标替换，是一种轻量级梯度修复策略。

Result: 在2D优化和文本到3D生成实验中，PixSDS显著减少了结构化伪影，同时保持了语义内容的完整性。

Conclusion: PixSDS通过VAE一致的梯度修复有效解决了潜在SDS的像素漂移问题，为文本到3D生成提供了一种无需重新训练的轻量级改进方案，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Score Distillation Sampling (SDS) enables text-to-3D generation by optimizing rendered images with a pretrained diffusion prior, but latent SDS often produces structured color artifacts and high-frequency texture noise. We identify a failure mode of latent SDS caused by VAE-induced pixel drift: the optimized image can move along pixel-space directions that are weakly constrained by the VAE encoder, so its latent representation remains clean and semantically meaningful while the image itself accumulates visible artifacts. We support this diagnosis with controlled 2D SDS experiments, VAE-only optimization, and a simplified analysis showing that encoder-like latent objectives can amplify image-space noise when the inverse mapping to pixels is underconstrained. Motivated by this observation, we propose PixSDS, a lightweight VAE-consistent gradient repair method. PixSDS decodes a latent SDS lookahead step and uses the decoded image as a clean direction for pixel-space optimization, reducing motion in VAE-inconsistent directions without retraining the diffusion model, changing the renderer, or replacing the SDS objective. Experiments in 2D optimization and text-to-3D generation show that PixSDS substantially reduces structured artifacts while preserving semantic content. Code is publicly available at https://sevashasla.github.io/pixsds-webpage/.

</details>


### [123] [Structure-aware Riemannian Growth Fields for 4D Plant Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13007)
*Meng-Yu Jennifer Kuo,Ryo Kawahara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的4D植物生长建模框架，通过将植物形态发生建模为结构感知的黎曼生长场上的连续程序化过程，从稀疏时间观测中重建植物连续的几何与拓扑演化。该方法将符号化生长规则嵌入连续测地流中，使器官发育遵循保持结构一致性的生物学调制轨迹，并贡献了一个10天双物种的密集注释数据集，实验证明其在几何精度和对应一致性上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D植物生长建模方法主要依赖密集配准，但由于扫描约束和自遮挡等原因难以获得可靠的密集序列，在大时间间隔下快速器官萌发违反局部刚性的场景中表现不佳。

Method: 将植物形态发生建模为结构感知的黎曼生长场上的连续程序化过程，联合建模拓扑演化和几何变形，保持植物学层级结构和跨远距离时间点的稳定时空对应；核心思想是将符号化生长规则嵌入连续测地流，使器官发育遵循生物学调制的轨迹以保持结构一致性。

Result: 贡献了一个10天双物种的密集几何和语义标注数据集。实验表明该方法能准确跟踪单个器官随时间生长的过程，在几何精度和对应一致性方面均显著优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 该框架成功克服了稀疏时间观测下的4D植物生长重建难题，通过将生物生长规则与连续几何流相结合，在拓扑变化下保持结构一致性，为植物形态发生建模提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a novel framework for 4D plant growth modeling that reconstructs the continuous geometric and topological evolution of plants from sparse temporal observations. Existing methods mainly rely on dense registration, yet reliable dense sequences are hard to obtain due to scanning constraints and self-occlusions, leaving these approaches struggling under large temporal gaps where rapid organ emergence violates local rigidity. To overcome this, we bridge these gaps by formulating plant morphogenesis as a continuous procedural process on a structure-aware Riemannian growth field; this jointly models topology evolution and geometric deformation, preserving botanical hierarchies and stable spatio-temporal correspondences across distant timepoints. Our key idea is to ground symbolic growth rules within a continuous geodesic flow, where organ development follows biologically modulated trajectories that preserve structural coherence under topological changes. We further contribute a 10-day dual-species dataset with dense geometric and semantic annotations. Experiments demonstrate that our method accurately tracks individual organ growth over time and significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both geometric accuracy and correspondence consistency.

</details>


### [124] [EgoPHI: Estimating Contact and Force from Egocentric Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13014)
*Andela Ilic,Rachel Schuchert,Yijing Jiang,Christian Holz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个从单目RGB图像和物体几何联合估计手-物体稠密接触图和三维力分布的方法EgoPHI。通过物理仿真管线生成稠密逐顶点力监督数据，解决真实力标注缺乏的问题。在分布内/外、仿真/真实数据集上验证了方法的有效性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从自我中心视角理解手-物体交互对于建模人类与周围世界的物理互动至关重要。然而，基于物理的交互推理需要估计作用于手和物体上的力，而不仅仅是定位接触点。现有方法缺乏可扩展的真实力标注数据，且力估计多限于图像空间或平面场景。

Method: 1) 提出基于物理的仿真管线，对现有手-物体数据集添加稠密逐顶点力监督；2) 在交互的手和可动关节物体网格上学习稠密3D接触与力分布，将基于视觉的力估计从图像空间/平面场景扩展到3D；3) 构建两个能捕捉稠密物体接触和力大小的物理实体，用于监督仿真到真实迁移评估。

Result: 在分布内和分布外基准上，EgoPHI相比现有方法提升了力估计精度，并能泛化到未见数据集。仿真到真实迁移实验中，在多样触摸和抓取类型下，EgoPHI在仿真、分布外和真实场景中都能恢复有意义的3D接触和力分布。

Conclusion: EgoPHI推动自我中心手-物体理解从接触定位迈向基于物理的交互推理，实现了跨越仿真到真实的稠密3D接触与力估计。

Abstract: Understanding hand-object interaction from egocentric vision is essential for modeling how people physically engage with the surrounding world. Yet reasoning about physically grounded interaction requires estimating the forces acting on hands and objects, beyond localizing contact. We present EgoPHI, the first method that jointly estimates dense contact maps and 3D force distributions on hand and object meshes from a single monocular RGB image and object geometry. To address the lack of scalable ground-truth force annotations, we introduce a physics-based simulation pipeline that augments existing hand-object datasets with dense per-vertex force supervision. EgoPHI then learns dense 3D contact and force on interacting hand and articulated object meshes, extending vision-based force estimation beyond image-space or planar settings. Our evaluation on in-distribution and out-of-distribution benchmarks shows that EgoPHI improves force estimation over existing approaches while generalizing to unseen datasets. To evaluate sim-to-real transfer, we constructed two physical objects that capture dense object contact and force magnitude and used them to record a dataset of interactions from eight participants across diverse touch and grasp types. Our results demonstrate that EgoPHI recovers meaningful 3D contact and force distributions in simulated, out-of-distribution, and real-world settings, advancing egocentric hand-object understanding from contact localization toward physically grounded interaction reasoning.

</details>


### [125] [RGB-D Video Generation for Improving Human-to-Robot Object Handover Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13028)
*Tianyu Sun,Zhoujie Fu,Zihui Gao,Bang Zhang,Guosheng Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Hand2Bot数据集的生成框架PassGen，该框架利用视频扩散模型和意图感知时间面部编码器为H2R物体交接场景合成逼真的RGB-D视频序列，并结合基于形态学的深度编辑策略来弥合仿真与真实传感器噪声的差距。实验表明该方法在意图识别精度、低误触发率及零样本机器人部署中均优于传统基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: H2R物体交接是人机协作的基础能力，但其发展受限于两方面阻碍：缺乏大规模、以人为中心的数据集；以及仿真与真实世界之间显著的sim-to-real差距。现有的数据收集方式难以覆盖丰富的上下文信息（如身体姿态、面部表情），且生成数据与物理传感器数据存在分布差异。

Method: 本文提出两个核心组件：(1) Hand2Bot，一个RGB-D视频数据集，包含交接场景中身体姿态、面部表情等丰富上下文信息及真实噪声模式；(2) PassGen，一个生成管线，利用稳定视频扩散（stable video diffusion）和一个意图感知时间面部编码器（Intention-Aware Temporal Face Encoder）来合成逼真的交接序列，并保证手-物一致性。此外，采用基于形态学的深度编辑策略（morphology-based depth editing）模拟物理深度图中真实存在的传感器噪声，以弥合sim-to-real差距。

Result: 在消融实验和实体机器人平台部署中，该框架实现了高意图识别精度和低误触发率。在PassGen训练下，模型具备鲁棒的零样本迁移能力和更早的意图预判能力，在共享工作空间中表现出有效的社交感知机器人行为。

Conclusion: 训练于PassGen生成数据的方法相比传统以手部为中心的基线，不仅能实现鲁棒的零样本迁移，还能更早地预测人类意图，从而支持机器人具备社交感知能力，验证了生成式数据增强在机器人交接任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Human-to-robot (H2R) object handover is a fundamental capability for human-robot collaboration, yet progress is hindered by the scarcity of large-scale, human-centric datasets and the significant sim-to-real gap. To address these challenges, we introduce Hand2Bot, an RGB-D video dataset that provides rich contextual information such as body posture and facial expressions, specifically collected for handover scenarios with real-world noise patterns. We further propose PassGen, a generative pipeline that leverages stable video diffusion and an Intention-Aware Temporal Face Encoder to synthesize realistic handover sequences while ensuring hand-object consistency. To bridge the sim-to-real gap, we implement a morphology-based depth editing strategy that replicates realistic sensor noise found in physical depth maps. Experimental evaluations demonstrate that our framework achieves high intention identification accuracy and low false trigger rates in both ablation studies and real-world deployment on a physical robot platform. Our results confirm that training on PassGen allows for robust zero-shot transfer and earlier intention anticipation compared to traditional hand-centric baselines, effectively enabling socially aware robotic behavior in shared workspaces.

</details>


### [126] [UniTraffic-Agent: Unified Traffic Video Reasoning for AI City Challenge 2026 Track 3 with Two Out-of-Domain Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13031)
*Peng Li,Qianqian Xu,Shilong Bao,Yangbangyan Jiang,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniTraffic-Agent系统，用于AI City Challenge Track 3的三个交通视频理解任务（交通异常推理TAR、鱼眼交通事件FETV和行人意图推理PSI-VQA）。系统采用"观察-推理-行动-验证"的工作流程，对视频帧进行时间戳采样，在同一请求中推理同一片段的所有问题，并通过任务特定适配器转换输出。在官方公共排行榜上分别排名TAR第16名（0.5780）、FETV第2名（0.4884）、PSI-VQA第4名（64.4161）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通视频理解对智能交通系统至关重要，需要解释交通事故如何发生、为何发生以及交互何时发生。然而，由于交通视频包含稀疏事件和多样视角，多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在此任务上仍面临困难。

Method: UniTraffic-Agent采用observe-reason-act-verify工作流：首先对带时间戳的视觉证据进行采样，然后对同一片段的所有问题在单次请求中进行统一推理，最后通过任务特定的动作适配器将响应转换为格式化的输出。

Result: 在官方公开排行榜上，MR-CAS在TAR任务上排名第16（得分0.5780），在FETV任务上排名第2（得分0.4884），在PSI-VQA任务上排名第4（得分64.4161）。

Conclusion: UniTraffic-Agent在三个任务上均取得有竞争力的成绩，特别是在FETV（第2名）和PSI-VQA（第4名）上表现突出，验证了所提出的工作流程和适配器策略在多任务交通视频理解中的有效性。

Abstract: Traffic video understanding has become an important problem in intelligent transportation, as road videos provide direct evidence for accidents, violations, and interactions between vehicles and vulnerable road users. A useful system should explain how a traffic event develops, why it happens, and when the relevant interaction occurs, yet this remains difficult for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) because traffic videos contain sparse events and varied viewpoints. We introduce UniTraffic-Agent, the MR-CAS solution for Track~3 of the 10th AI City Challenge, which includes Traffic Anomaly Reasoning (TAR) and two out-of-domain evaluations: FETV for fisheye traffic events and PSI-VQA for pedestrian intention reasoning. UniTraffic-Agent follows an observe--reason--act--verify workflow that samples timestamped visual evidence, reasons over all questions from the same clip in one request, and converts responses through task-specific action adapters. On the official Public leaderboards, MR-CAS ranks 16th on TAR with a score of 0.5780, 2nd on FETV with 0.4884, and 4th on PSI-VQA with 64.4161. The code is available at https://github.com/Roclp/UniTraffic-Agent.

</details>


### [127] [Spatially-Grounded Text-to-Video Generation via Inference-Time Gradient-Free Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13037)
*Guillaume Jeanneret,Mathis Koroglu,Hugo Caselles-Dupré,Arnaud Dapogny,Matthieu Cord*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GATO-Vid是一种新颖的无训练、无梯度的视频生成空间可控方法，通过提出替代交叉注意力分数并解析求解得到闭式解，配合在线注入机制实现精确的空间引导，在定位精度上显著优于现有基线且计算开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Diffusion Transformer文本到视频模型虽具备出色的合成质量，但细粒度空间可控性仍是重大挑战。现有无训练方法依赖基于梯度的优化技术，产生高昂的计算开销，这一瓶颈在大规模架构中被进一步放大。

Method: 提出GATO-Vid框架：1）引入替代交叉注意力分数并解析求解，获得精确的闭式解，避免昂贵的反向传播；2）设计针对Transformer潜在空间拓扑流形的在线注入机制，将解析解无缝集成到生成过程中。

Result: 实验表明GATO-Vid在定位精度上显著优于现有基线方法，同时计算开销极低，克服了传统梯度优化方法的效率瓶颈。

Conclusion: GATO-Vid证明了解析式轨迹优化可以替代梯度下降用于视频生成中的空间可控性，在保持精确空间定位能力的同时实现了近零额外计算成本，为大规模Diffusion Transformer模型的细粒度控制提供了高效路径。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformer Text-to-Video models have achieved remarkable synthesis quality, yet fine-grained spatial controllability remains a significant challenge.
