This project asks whether feeding a report into a knowledge-graph retrieval system, Microsoft GraphRAG, rather than a standard vector-similarity retrieval system (Naive RAG), produces detection plans that rely more on these durable, top-of-pyramid clues. Both systems are given the same report, the same generation instructions, and the same language model to write the final plan; only the retrieval step differs. In a detailed case study of one APT28 report, the GraphRAG plan kept firing at 100\% of its detections after every IP address, domain, and file hash in the report was rotated, while the Naive RAG plan kept firing at only 29\%. Repeating the comparison across nine real CTI reports from four vendors confirms the same pattern: GraphRAG plans consistently reach higher, harder-to-evade levels of the pyramid, even when the two systems end up close on total score. The results support treating knowledge-graph-aware retrieval as the architecturally correct foundation for automatically generating SOC-deployable hunting plans, while showing that the wording of the generation prompt matters almost as much as the retrieval back-end itself.

</details>


### [194] [A Commitment-Based Hybrid Post-Quantum Cryptographic Model for Multi-File Cloud Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13138)
*Lemdi Frank Prikutse,Regina Esi Turkson,Alimatu-Saadia Yussiff,Abdul-Lateef Yussiff,Maame G. Asante-Mensah*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于承诺的混合后量子云存储认证模型，通过SHA3-256批量承诺将所有密文绑定到一个固定大小摘要，仅需一次混合签名（Ed25519+ML-DSA-65），将每批次后量子签名调用次数从n次降至1次。实验表明，在n=1000时，签名阶段耗时较每文件双签名基线分别降低629、606、725倍（对应100KB、1MB、10MB文件）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云存储客户端需要能抵御未来量子攻击的认证方案，常采用经典原语与标准化后量子替代方案混合的构造。然而，朴素扩展到多文件上传时，每个文件都需一次格基签名，签名成本占比过高，在实际批量规模下成为瓶颈。

Method: 提出基于承诺的混合后量子模型，包含AES-256-GCM批量加密、X25519与ML-KEM-768混合密钥封装机制、Ed25519与ML-DSA-65混合双签名。核心思想是使用SHA3-256对整批密文计算批量承诺，生成固定大小摘要，仅对该摘要签名一次，将签名次数从每文件一次降为每批一次；其余加密和哈希操作受快速对称吞吐量限制。

Result: 在商用客户端平台上，20次重复平均，随着批量增大签名阶段耗时保持近恒定，而逐文件的基线呈线性增长。在n=1000时，对于100KB、1MB、10MB文件，签名阶段耗时相比共享其他所有原语的逐文件双签名基线分别降低629、606和725倍。

Conclusion: 批量承诺混合后量子模型有效消除了多文件上传场景中后量子签名的批量规模瓶颈，签名成本与批量大小解耦，在保持混合安全性的同时显著提升认证效率。

Abstract: Cloud storage clients increasingly require authentication that remains secure against future quantum-capable adversaries, motivating hybrid constructions that combine classical primitives with standardized post-quantum alternatives. Extended naively to multi-file upload, such constructions incur a per-file lattice signing cost that dominates authentication time and becomes prohibitive at realistic batch sizes. This paper presents a commitment-based hybrid post-quantum model that addresses this bottleneck. It comprises AES-256-GCM bulk encryption, a hybrid X25519 with ML-KEM-768 key encapsulation mechanism, and a hybrid Ed25519 with ML-DSA-65 dual signature, computed over a SHA3-256 batch commitment. The commitment binds all ciphertexts in a batch to a single fixed-size digest that is signed once, reducing the number of post-quantum signature invocations per batch from n to one, independent of batch size; the remaining encryption and hashing is bounded by fast symmetric throughput. On a commodity client platform, averaged over 20 repetitions, this holds signing-phase time near-constant as the batch grows while the per-file baseline scales linearly. At n = 1000, the model reduces signing-phase time by factors of 629, 606, and 725 for 100 KB, 1 MB, and 10 MB files respectively, against a per-file dual-signing baseline sharing every other primitive.

</details>


### [195] [Smart Contract Invariants Protect Against Cybercriminals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13191)
*Sofia Bobadilla,Humaira Afrin,Angela Novelli,Martin Monperrus*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出智能合约不变量是应对区块链网络攻击最有力的解决方案，并构建了包含28个真实以太坊攻击案例的基准INVARIANTEVAL，通过PONDEREPLAY框架验证这些不变量能阻断所有攻击，在108,637笔历史交易上得到了完全验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链是计算领域对抗性最强的环境之一，数十亿美元被利用漏洞的网络罪犯窃取，但至今没有概念或技术被证明能真正解决问题。本文认为经典的程序不变量概念可能是最强大的解决方案，因此需要系统性地验证不变量能否抵御真实世界中的攻击。

Method: 设计了一个原创的实验协议：(1)研究不变量如何在过去的真实攻击中提供保护；(2)考察最先进的自动化工具能否发现这些不变量。具体而言，构建了INVARIANTEVAL基准，包含28个真实以太坊漏洞利用案例，每个案例配有人工编写且能阻断攻击的不变量；所有不变量通过PONDEREPLAY重放框架验证——该框架重放历史交易以证明智能合约不变量的正确性和健全性。

Result: 智能合约不变量成功阻断了INVARIANTEVAL中所有28个网络犯罪攻击案例，并通过重放108,637笔历史交易得到了完整验证，证明不变量的正确性和健全性。

Conclusion: 大规模的实验清楚地表明，智能合约不变量能够有效抵御网络犯罪攻击，为区块链安全问题提供了一个有前景的解决方向。

Abstract: Blockchains are among the most adversarial environments in computing. Billions are stolen by cybercriminals who exploit vulnerabilities. This is an open problem and no concept or technique has proven to really make a difference. In this paper, we claim that the classical notion of program invariant is perhaps the most powerful solution to the problem. We devise anoriginal experimental protocol to 1) study how invariants would have protected against past real-world attacks and 2) whether state-of-the-art automated tools can find them. The experimental toolchain is sophisticated. It is based on INVARIANTEVAL, a benchmark of 28 real Ethereum exploits, each paired with a human-authored invariant that blocks the attack. We validate every invariant with PONDEREPLAY, a replay framework that re-executes transactions in order to prove the correctness and soundness of smart contract invariants. We demonstrate that smart contract invariants block all the cybercriminal attacks in INVARIANTEVAL, fully validated by replaying 108,637 historical transactions. Our large-scale experiments clearly demonstrate that smart contract invariants protect against cybercriminals.

</details>


### [196] [Slow and Steady: Preventing MEV with Verifiable Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13271)
*Zeta Avarikioti,Dimitris Karakostas,Karl Kreder,Shreekara Shastry*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种防御机制，通过在交易生成时强制执行可验证延迟（Verifiable Delay），阻止区块创建者对MEV机会做出及时反应，从而在不破坏活性的前提下抵御最大可提取价值（MEV）攻击。论文在拜占庭设置和理性参与者博弈论模型中均给出正面结果，同时给出负面界限，并基于历史MEV数据验证了该机制的实际可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MEV（最大可提取价值）是分布式账本中的重大威胁，区块生产者可以在看到MEV机会后通过重新排序或插入交易来提取价值，损害普通用户利益。现有防御手段（如加密内存池、阈值加密等）存在信任假设或活性问题，亟需一种不依赖额外信任假设的机制来限制区块创建者对MEV机会的及时反应能力。

Method: 提出在交易生成阶段强制执行可验证延迟（VDF）的机制，使得区块创建者在看到MEV机会出现时无法在不违反活性承诺的前提下及时介入。该机制在拜占庭容错模型和基于理性的博弈论模型下分别进行分析，并推导出该防御思路的能力上限（负面界限）。最后结合真实历史MEV数据进行实证评估。

Result: 在拜占庭设置和理性模型下均取得正面结果，证明该机制能有效限制MEV攻击；同时给出负面界限，明确了此类防御手段的局限性；历史数据实证表明该机制能现实地阻止大多数现有MEV威胁。

Conclusion: 基于可验证延迟的防御机制为MEV问题提供了新的解决思路，在降低对信任假设依赖的同时能够有效抑制大部分MEV攻击，尽管存在固有局限性，但仍具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Our work presents a defense mechanism against Maximal Extractable Value (MEV) opportunities in distributed ledgers. The mechanism relies on the idea of enforcing a verifiable delay when generating transactions, such that a block creator cannot react to the appearance of a MEV opportunity without breaking liveness. We present positive results both in the Byzantine setting and in a game theoretic model of rational participants. We additionally present negative bounds that outline the limitations of this line of defense. Finally, we explore real-world implementation details of verifiable delays and show that, based on historical MEV data, our mechanism could realistically help prevent most existing MEV threats.

</details>


### [197] [VR-Themis: A Scalable Framework for Virtual Reality Application Clone Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13290)
*Gengyang Xu,Hanyang Guo,Hong-Ning Dai,Weizhi Meng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出VR-Themis，一个针对虚拟现实（VR）应用克隆检测的两阶段框架。该框架利用HOB（层-对象-行为）度量，先通过粗粒度阶段基于统计特征对应用进行聚类以实现规模化，再通过细粒度阶段对可疑应用进行深入的相似度分析。实验在4,277个VR应用中成功检测出307个疑似克隆应用且无误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的移动应用克隆检测方法主要针对Android等移动平台设计，未能捕捉VR应用的核心特征，因此无法有效检测VR市场中的克隆应用。非法用户频繁在VR市场中投放克隆应用，威胁用户安全隐私并侵犯开发者版权，亟需针对VR应用特征的专用检测方法。

Method: 提出两阶段检测框架VR-Themis，基于HOB（Hierarchy-Object-Behaviour）度量。第一阶段为粗粒度聚类阶段，利用可检索的统计特征对应用进行聚类，使其可扩展到大规模VR应用数据集；第二阶段为细粒度分析阶段，针对第一阶段识别出的可疑应用，使用自定义的HOB度量计算相似度，进行深度分析。

Result: 在收集的4,277个VR应用中，VR-Themis成功检测出307个疑似克隆应用，且没有产生任何误报，验证了该方法的有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: VR-Themis通过结合粗粒度聚类与细粒度分析的两阶段策略，利用VR特有的HOB度量，能够高效且准确地检测VR应用克隆，为VR生态系统的安全与版权保护提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Repackaging of mobile applications (aka app cloning) not only threatens the security and privacy of mobile users but also infringes upon the copyright of the original app developers. However, existing detection methods that primarily focus on mobile platforms (such as Android) fail to capture the essential features of virtual reality (VR). Consequently, they are inadequate for effectively detecting cloned VR apps, which have often been targeted by illegal users in the VR market. Considering the unique features of VR apps, this paper proposes a two-stage app clone detection framework, namely VR-Themis, based on \emph{Hierarchy-Object-Behaviour} (HOB). Firstly, VR-Themis exploits the coarse-grained stage to cluster apps based on their retrievable statistical features, making this tool scalable to large-scale VR app datasets. Then, in the fine-grained stage, VR-Themis performs in-depth analysis of the suspicious apps (identified in the first stage) by calculating similarity using our defined \emph{HOB metrics}. Our extensive experiments indicate that VR-Themis successfully detects 307 suspected clone apps from the collected 4,277 VR apps without false positives, demonstrating its effectiveness and scalability.

</details>


### [198] [TeleGapper: On the (un)reliability of Privacy Policies in Telegram Mini apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13390)
*Luca Ferrari,Mariano Ceccato,Luca Verderame*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TeleGapper，一个黑盒动态分析框架，用于评估Telegram Mini Apps的隐私态势。通过对278个Mini Apps的分析，发现59.4%联系了未披露的第三方，78.8%完全依赖Telegram默认隐私政策，且无一提供同意或退出机制，揭示了该生态系统中严重的透明度和合规性缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Telegram Mini Apps作为嵌入Telegram客户端中的Web应用，虽然使用广泛且能访问Telegram提供的上下文，但其隐私属性几乎未被探索。与WeChat等采用严格控制、专有执行框架的生态系统不同，Telegram Mini Apps在WebView中运行，结合了平台上下文、标准Web能力和不受限制的出站网络，可通过普通Web请求向分析、广告、追踪等第三方传输敏感信息，且可视性有限。因此需要系统性地评估这些应用的隐私披露和实际数据流之间的一致性。

Method: TeleGapper采用黑盒动态分析框架，通过捕获运行时网络流量、识别第三方通信、并将观测到的数据流与披露的隐私信息进行对比，来评估Mini Apps的隐私态势。研究从社区驱动的目录tApps Center收集了278个可用的Mini Apps作为评估样本。

Result: 研究发现：59.4%的Mini Apps至少联系了一个未披露的第三方；78.8%完全依赖Telegram提供的默认隐私政策；没有任何一个Mini App提供用户同意或退出机制。这些结果揭示了广泛使用但研究不足的生态系统中存在的实质性透明度和合规性缺口。

Conclusion: Telegram Mini Apps生态存在严重的隐私披露缺口，默认隐私政策机制无法准确反映各Mini App的实际数据实践，且缺乏用户选择机制。该研究凸显了此生态系统对隐私合规监管的需求，并为后续改进Telegram Mini Apps的隐私披露机制提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Telegram Mini Apps are Web applications embedded within the Telegram client, forming an ecosystem of third-party services within one of the world's most widely used messaging platforms. Despite their growing adoption and access to Telegram-provided context, their privacy properties remain largely unexplored. Unlike ecosystems such as WeChat, which rely on tightly controlled, proprietary execution frameworks, Telegram adopts a different model: Mini Apps run inside a WebView, combining platform-provided context with standard Web capabilities and unrestricted outbound networking. This enables applications to transmit sensitive information to analytics, advertising, tracking, or other third parties through ordinary Web requests, often with limited visibility.
  Privacy disclosures are therefore critical for transparency. Telegram allows Mini Apps either to define an application-specific privacy policy or to rely on a platform-provided default policy. While the latter reduces the developer's disclosure burden, it may lead to generic statements that do not accurately capture actual data practices of individual Mini Apps.
  In this paper, we present TeleGapper, a black-box dynamic analysis framework to assess the privacy posture of Mini Apps by capturing runtime network traffic, identifying third-party communications, and comparing observed data flows against disclosed privacy information. We evaluate 278 working Mini Apps collected from tApps Center, a community-driven catalogue for discovering third-party applications in Telegram. We find that 59.4% contact at least one undisclosed third party, 78.8% rely exclusively on Telegram's default privacy policy, and none provides a consent or opt-out mechanism. These findings expose a substantial transparency and compliance gap in a widely used yet understudied ecosystem.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [199] [Sinkhorn Linearization and the Spectral Proxy: Unifying the Statistical and Algorithmic Theory of Feature-Parameterized Inverse Optimal Transport via a Single Spectral Sandwich](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13201)
*Han Dong,Jiaming Li,Yongqiang Gong,Ruixi Li,Yin Liu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统建立了特征参数化代价函数下的逆最优传输（IOT）统计与算法理论。核心贡献是Sinkhorn线性化——熵OT计划对代价的隐函数敏感性——以及其谱代理公式。基于谱夹心不等式，导出了单一核心界，并在此基础上证明了四个定理（可辨识性、稀疏一致性、适定性、收敛性）和一个观察（误设定下的投影估计）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆最优传输问题缺乏统一的统计与算法理论框架，特别是当代价函数由特征参数化时，其可辨识性、估计一致性和算法收敛性缺乏系统性论证。需要建立从理论保证到实际算法的完整数学基础。

Method: 提出Sinkhorn线性化作为核心技术手段，通过隐函数定理刻画熵OT计划对代价参数的敏感性；引入谱代理公式使分析既在谱意义上精确又在几何上透明。利用受限Hessian的谱夹心界推导核心下界，并据此分别建立可辨识性、sparsistency、适定性与收敛性定理。

Result: 建立了四个定理和一个观察：可辨识性（theta在规范核商空间上全局单射，维度界F≤(K-1)^2）；稀疏一致性（l1惩罚估计在可表示性与分数集中条件下指数概率恢复真实支撑）；适定性（特征矩映射强单调，逆映射Lipschitz连续）；收敛性（局部强凸保证梯度下降单调收敛）；误设定下估计收敛到OT模型投影，投影映射的Hölder连续性由数值实验评估得到经验指数alpha_eff∈(0,1)。

Conclusion: 该工作为特征参数化逆最优传输提供了自洽、自足的统计与算法理论，核心界sigma_min≥(pi_min/(a_max ε))√(λ_min(Σ))作为驱动全部分析的统一工具，为后续IOT研究奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We develop the statistical and algorithmic theory of inverse optimal transport (IOT) under the feature-parameterized cost C_theta(i,j) = -theta^T phi(i,j). The core technical contribution is the Sinkhorn linearization -- the implicit-function sensitivity of the entropic OT plan to the cost -- together with its spectral proxy, a formula that is spectrally exact yet geometrically transparent.
  The restricted Hessian on the tangent space satisfies the spectral sandwich (pi_min/epsilon) I <= H_T^{-1} <= (pi_max/epsilon) I, yielding the single core bound sigma_min >= (pi_min/(a_max epsilon)) sqrt(lambda_min(Sigma)) that drives the entire theory. On this core we establish four theorems and one observation.
  T1 (identifiability): theta is globally injective on the quotient of the gauge kernel, with dimension bound F <= (K-1)^2. T2 (sparsistency): the l1-penalized estimator recovers the true support under irrepresentability and score concentration, with exponential failure probability. T3 (well-posedness): the feature-moment map M(theta) = Phi^T x_theta is strongly monotone, and the inverse is Lipschitz with constant L <= epsilon ||Phi^T S_a||_op / (pi_min lambda_min(Sigma)). T4 (convergence): local strong convexity with mu >= pi_min^2 lambda_min(Sigma) / epsilon^2 guarantees monotone gradient descent convergence. O5 (misspecification): the estimator converges to the OT-model projection of the truth; the Holder continuity of the projection map is assessed numerically, yielding setting-dependent empirical exponents alpha_eff in (0,1).

</details>


### [200] [Black-Box Knowledge Transfer across Distinct Feature Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12403)
*Oh-Ran Kwon,Daeyoung Ham*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从预训练黑盒预测函数向异构输入空间迁移预测知识的方法，将目标回归函数分解为可迁移与不可迁移两部分，通过两步神经网络过程估计，并证明了其预测风险界优于非迁移方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练黑盒函数蕴含了从海量数据中提炼的知识，但当可用输入特征与黑盒期望的特征不一致（异构）时，无法直接使用。已有的迁移学习方法通常假设输入空间同构，本研究旨在解决黑盒知识向异构新输入空间迁移的问题。

Method: 将目标回归函数分解为可迁移成分（由黑盒提供信息）与不可迁移成分（捕获新空间的独特信息）。提出两步神经网络程序：第一步利用丰富的无标注特征对（连接两个输入空间）估计可迁移成分；第二步利用有限的标注数据估计不可迁移成分。并扩展至多个黑盒场景，通过聚合降低预测误差。

Result: 推导了预测风险界，证明在不可迁移成分较小或平滑时优于非迁移方案，且方法能自适应两种情形。在额外条件下，所估计器的最坏情况风险严格小于仅用标注数据估计的极小极大风险（多项式阶更低）。多黑盒聚合可降低预测误差。模拟与真实数据验证了方法实用性。

Conclusion: 本文为异构输入空间下的黑盒知识迁移提供了理论保障与实用算法，填补了该领域空白，并展示了多黑盒聚合的额外收益。

Abstract: Pre-trained black-box predictive functions encode knowledge distilled from massive datasets and extensive computation. However, when the available input features differ from those the black box expects, direct use is infeasible. We introduce a method for transferring predictive knowledge from the black box to a new, heterogeneous input space. Our approach decomposes the target regression function into a transferable component, which the black box can inform, and a non-transferable component, which captures information unique to the new space. We propose a two-step neural network procedure, estimating the transferable component from abundant unlabeled feature pairs that bridge the two input spaces and the non-transferable component from limited labels. We derive prediction risk bounds that improve on those of a non-transfer alternative when the non-transferable component is small or smooth, and the procedure adapts to either case. Under additional conditions, the worst-case risk of our estimator is of strictly smaller polynomial order than the minimax risk of estimation from the labeled data alone. We extend the framework to multiple black boxes, each on its own input space, and show that aggregation can reduce prediction error relative to the best single black box. Simulated and real data demonstrate the practical value of the method.

</details>


### [201] [SSPO: Structure-Aware Similarity-Weighted Preference Optimization for Neural Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12443)
*Yuanyu Li,Jintao Xu,Zijiang Liu,Yongzhi Qi,Ningxuan Kang,Jianshen Zhang,Wei Qi,Chen Xie,Zuo-Jun Max Shen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出SSPO（结构感知相似度加权偏好优化）方法，通过非对称加权的留一法基线联合评分所有B个采样解，同时解决现有神经组合优化（NCO）训练方法中的"梯度信号极化"和"基线冗余"两大失败。实验在TSP、EFL、JSP基准上持续优于先前的best-anchor和均匀权重基线，并与均匀RLOO的对比证实结构感知加权是改进的主要驱动力。SSPO训练的EFL策略已在京东生产级设施选址系统中部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经组合优化依赖并行解采样进行训练，但现有方法未能充分利用共同采样解组中蕴含的丰富信息。偏好优化方法锚定单一最优解而丢弃其他同伴解的细粒度质量与结构信号（梯度信号极化）；均值基线则均匀加权同伴，导致结构近似相同的解以冗余信息淹没基线并保持高梯度方差（基线冗余）。两种失败模式均源自对解组结构信息的利用不足。

Method: 提出SSPO框架：设计非对称加权的留一法（leave-one-out）基线，对所有B个采样解联合评分，使结构上不同的解获得更高权重。该基线基于零参数的、问题自适应的解嵌入，由编码器已有的节点表示直接构建，无需额外可学习参数。

Result: 在TSP、EFL和JSP基准上的实验表明，SSPO一致性地优于先前的best-anchor和均匀权重基线。通过与TSP和EFL上的均匀RLOO直接对比，证实结构感知加权是改进的主要驱动因素。此外，SSPO训练的EFL策略已在京东生产级设施选址系统中成功部署，验证了其规模化实用可行性。

Conclusion: SSPO通过单一机制同时解决了梯度信号极化和基线冗余问题，结构感知的相似度加权是提升NCO训练效果的关键因素。该方法不仅在多个组合优化基准上取得一致的性能提升，还在京东生产系统中证明了工业级实用价值。

Abstract: Neural combinatorial optimization (NCO) relies on parallel solution sampling for training, yet existing methods fail to fully exploit the rich information latent in a co-sampled solution group. Preference-optimization methods anchor on the single best solution and discard fine-grained quality and structural signal from all other peers-a failure we term gradient signal polarization. Mean-based baselines instead weight peers uniformly, so structurally near-identical peers flood the baseline with redundant information and keep gradient variance high-a failure we term baseline redundancy. We propose SSPO (Structure-Aware Similarity-Weighted Preference Optimization), which scores all $B$ sampled solutions jointly through a dissimilarity-weighted leave-one-out baseline: structurally distinct peers receive higher weight, resolving both failures in a single mechanism. The baseline uses zero-parameter, problem-adaptive solution embeddings built from the encoder's existing node representations. Experiments on TSP, EFL, and JSP benchmarks show consistent gains over prior best-anchor and uniform-weight baselines. A direct comparison against uniform RLOO on TSP and EFL confirms that structure-aware weighting is the primary driver of improvement. The SSPO-trained EFL policy has been deployed in a production facility-location system at JD$\mathord{.}$com, confirming practical viability at scale.

</details>


### [202] [Online Inference for Quantile Temporal Difference Learning in Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12973)
*Zijie Cheng,Yang Peng,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究分布强化学习中分位数时序差分学习（QTD）的统计推断问题。在生成模型可访问的假设下，为同步和异步QTD建立了函数中心极限定理，证明其平均迭代弱收敛于重标度布朗运动。基于随机缩放的在线推断方法构建渐近枢轴统计量，无需存储完整QTD轨迹即可在线计算，大幅降低内存需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布强化学习中的QTD算法在实际应用中需要进行统计推断，但现有研究缺乏对QTD的严格统计推断理论基础。传统的推断方法通常需要存储完整迭代轨迹，这在内存上不可行。因此需要建立QTD的函数中心极限定理，并设计高效、在线可计算的推断方法。

Method: 1) 在生成模型假设下，为同步和异步QTD分别建立函数中心极限定理，证明平均迭代弱收敛于重标度Brownian motion；2) 基于随机缩放（random scaling）思想，利用QTD路径的全程信息构建渐近枢轴统计量；3) 该统计量支持在线计算，无需存储整个QTD迭代轨迹，大幅减少内存开销。

Result: 建立了同步和异步QTD的函数中心极限定理，并提出了基于随机缩放的在线推断方法。该统计量是渐近枢轴的，可以在线计算且仅需常数量级的内存，显著优于需要存储完整轨迹的传统推断方案。

Conclusion: 本文为分布强化学习中的QTD提供了严格的统计推断理论基础，同时提出的在线推断方法兼顾了统计有效性和计算效率，为分布强化学习中的不确定性量化提供了实用的工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we study how to perform statistical inference for quantile temporal difference learning (QTD) in distributional reinforcement learning. Assuming access to a generative model, we first establish functional central limit theorems for both synchronous and asynchronous QTD, which show that the averaged iterates of QTD converge weakly to a rescaled Brownian motion. We next provide online inference methods. Based on random scaling, the inference procedure constructs an asymptotically pivotal statistic for inference by using the information along the whole QTD path. Meanwhile, the proposed statistic can be computed online without storing the entire trajectory of QTD iterates. This substantially reduces the memory requirement and enables efficient statistical inference in distributional reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [203] [Statistical Properties of Robust Learning under Distributional Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13133)
*Zhiyi Li,Xiaojie Mao,Yunbei Xu,Ruohan Zhan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布偏移下DRO（分布鲁棒优化）和RS（鲁棒满足）两类鲁棒学习方法在目标环境（偏移后的分布）中的有限样本泛化误差，推导了相应的理论界，并提出基于部分偏移信息（幅度或方向）的超参数校准方法，最后将框架应用于网络批量规模优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒学习框架（DRO和RS）在分布偏移下的有限样本保证研究不足：已有分析要么只在源环境中建立保证，要么只针对模糊集上的对抗性最坏情况性能。缺乏对目标环境（偏移后的真实分布）下泛化误差的系统性刻画和两种方法的系统比较。

Method: 1) 首次为DRO和RS推导目标偏移环境下的有限样本泛化误差界，显式刻画对偏移敏感性降低与鲁棒超参数带来的正则化惩罚之间的权衡，并避免Wasserstein经验集中的维度灾难；2) 在部分偏移信息（幅度或方向）可用时，提出信息导向的超参数校准方法，并在相同信息条件下对两种方法进行比较；3) 将框架应用于网络批量规模（lot-sizing）问题以验证和解释鲁棒策略对需求正向偏移的响应。

Result: 1) 得到了目标偏移环境下DRO和RS的有限样本泛化误差界；2) 在部分信息框架下，DRO和RS展现出互补的理论与实证行为；3) 网络批量规模问题的应用揭示了鲁棒策略对正向需求偏移的响应机制。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了鲁棒学习方法在分布偏移下统计性质理解的空白，为DRO和RS的系统比较提供了原则性基础。

Abstract: Distributional shifts arise when the target deployment environment differs from the source environment that generated the training data. Robust learning frameworks such as Distributionally Robust Optimization (DRO) and Robust Satisficing (RS) aim to address this challenge, yet their finite-sample guarantees under such shifts, and their systematic comparison, remain underexplored: existing analyses typically establish guarantees either in the source environment or for adversarial worst-case performance over an ambiguity set. This paper instead studies generalization error in the target environment---the excess loss under the shifted target distribution. Our contributions are threefold. First, we derive finite-sample generalization error bounds in the shifted target environment for both DRO and RS. These bounds explicitly characterize the trade-off between reduced sensitivity to shift and the regularization penalty induced by each method's robustness hyperparameter, and they avoid the curse of dimensionality associated with Wasserstein empirical concentration. Second, when partial shift information such as shift magnitude or direction is available, we propose information-directed hyperparameter calibrations and compare the two methods given the same information. Under these calibrations, and in the partial-information regimes we study, DRO and RS exhibit complementary theoretical and empirical behavior. Finally, we apply the framework to a network lot-sizing problem, using it to interpret how robust policies respond to positive shifts in the demand distribution. Together, these results fill a gap in understanding the statistical properties of robust learning methods under distributional shifts and provide a principled basis for comparing DRO and RS.

</details>


### [204] [High-dimensional networks and mean squared error for possibly misspecified models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13171)
*Lourens Waldorp*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究高维网络中的邻域选择问题，证明使用最小描述长度（MDL）作为惩罚项可以在高维设置下获得保守（低误报率）的邻域估计，而传统方法（如Lasso、AIC、BIC）会产生大量虚假边。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着网络分析纳入越来越多变量，高维场景（参数远多于观测）下如何准确估计每个节点的邻域（即存在哪些连接）成为关键问题。传统邻域选择方法在高维下存在高误报率问题，且非线性模型下高维惩罚的影响尚未被充分理解。

Method: 作者将邻域选择问题建模为线性模型估计，分析了岭参数对均方误差的影响及其与测试方差、边数量的关系。将高维邻域选择与机器学习中的"双下降"（double descent）现象建立联系，论证模型空间体积应纳入惩罚项，并证明在高维设置下最小描述长度（MDL）方法能够实现正确的邻域选择。

Result: 证明在高维设置下，MDL方法无论是真实模型为线性还是非线性（被错误假设为线性），都能获得正确的邻域选择或更小（低误报率）的结果。相比之下，Lasso、AIC、BIC等方法会产生高误报率的虚假边。

Conclusion: 传统的邻域选择方法在高维网络分析中存在局限性，MDL通过将模型空间体积纳入惩罚，为高维网络正确识别连接提供了理论基础和可行方案。

Abstract: To avoid missing important variables and their connections in networks, more and more variables are included in network analysis. Here we show that in a setting with many more parameters than observations (high-dimensional) it is possible to get a conservative (i.e., low false positive rate) estimate of the neighbourhood for each node (which connections are in the network). A neighbourhood is often estimated with a linear model, and this leads to two interesting cases: (i) If the true model is linear, then neighbourhood selection work reasonably well, and (ii) if the true model is nonlinear, then neighbourhood selection requires a penalty for the high dimensions. Here we show the impact of the ridge parameter on the mean squared error, and how this leads to low test variance and hence to neighbourhoods with large numbers of edges. We connect these insights with results from machine learning, where the so-called double descent (when more parameters are included than observations, the mean squared error goes down a second time) has put the traditional view on model selection upside down. Essentially, for adequate neighbourhood selection in models with a large number of parameters, the volume of the model space needs to be included in the penalty. Most neighbourhood selection methods (e.g., Lasso, AIC, BIC) lead to spurious edges (high false positive rate), but we prove that in the high-dimensional setting, minimum description length leads to correct neighbourhood selection or smaller (low false positive rates) in both cases when either the model is correctly or incorrectly assumed linear

</details>


### [205] [Wasserstein Filtering: A Sample Selection Method for Robust Distribution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13418)
*Yikai Xu,Zhao Chen,Jian Huang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Wasserstein Filtering (WF)，一种基于样本选择的框架，通过最大化所选子集经验分布与全量污染经验分布之间的Wasserstein距离，剔除几何上有影响的离群点，从而恢复潜在的干净数据分布。配套提出三种可计算算法（SinkMarg、SinkWF、SlicedWF），并在FELP污染模型下证明了该估计器在有限协方差分布族上的极小极大最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际数据中，部分样本可能被污染（如离群点或局部扰动），导致经验分布偏离真实干净的总体分布。现有方法往往难以在理论上刻画污染特性并高效地筛选出干净样本，因此需要一种模型无关、理论保证且计算可行的样本筛选框架来恢复底层干净分布。

Method: 提出Wasserstein Filtering框架：从含污染数据的经验分布中选取一个子集，使其经验分布与全量污染经验分布之间的Wasserstein距离最大化，从而优先隔离并移除几何上有影响力的离群点。为实现可计算性，设计了三种算法：边际筛选方案SinkMarg、基于熵正则最优传输的联合优化算法SinkWF、以及基于切片Wasserstein近似的联合优化算法SlicedWF。理论上引入FELP污染模型（远分离离群点+局部不可区分扰动），并在此模型下证明WF估计器在有限协方差分布族上达到极小极大最优。

Result: 在合成数据集、基准异常检测套件以及基于扩散模型的鲁棒生成学习任务上进行的大量数值实验表明，WF是一种高度实用、模型无关的预处理工具，具有有竞争力的离群点检测性能，并在重度污染场景下为生成建模提供了显著的下游收益。

Conclusion: WF提供了一种理论上保证、计算上可行且模型无关的样本筛选方法，能够有效地从含污染数据中恢复干净分布，同时兼顾离群点检测和鲁棒生成建模等下游任务，展示了良好的实用性与推广潜力。

Abstract: Given a dataset where a portion of the samples are contaminated, our goal is to recover the underlying clean population distribution. To this end, we propose Wasserstein Filtering (WF), a novel sample selection framework that discards a fraction of suspicious samples and estimates the target distribution using the empirical measure of the remaining data. The core insight is to select a subset of samples whose empirical distribution maximizes its Wasserstein distance to the fully contaminated empirical distribution, thereby preferentially isolating and removing geometrically influential outliers. To render this optimization computationally tractable, we introduce three algorithms: a marginal screening scheme, SinkMarg, and two joint optimization algorithms, SinkWF and SlicedWF, leveraging entropic optimal transport and sliced Wasserstein approximations, respectively. On the theoretical front, we introduce the Far Exclusion and Local Projection (FELP) contamination model, which characterizes corruptions consisting of well-separated outliers and locally indistinguishable perturbations. Under this model, we prove that the WF estimator achieves minimax optimality over distribution families with bounded covariance. Extensive numerical experiments on synthetic datasets, benchmark anomaly detection suites, and robust generative learning with diffusion models demonstrate that WF serves as a highly practical, model-agnostic preprocessing tool. It delivers competitive outlier detection performance and provides substantial downstream benefits for generative modeling under heavy contamination.

</details>


### [206] [Bagging Robustly Learns VC Classes with Linear Sample Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13514)
*Omar Montasser*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文重新研究了对抗性鲁棒学习问题，证明了VC类的对抗鲁棒可学习性其样本复杂度与VC维d呈线性关系，相比之前的上界实现了指数级改进。该结果通过结合bagging与鲁棒经验风险最小化（RERM）的简单不当算法实现，并伴随一个下界证明表明该算法在oracle模型中的查询次数是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Montasser等人（2019）给出的对抗鲁棒学习的样本复杂度上界与VC维呈指数关系，这在实际应用中难以接受。本文旨在探索是否存在更高效的算法，使得对抗鲁棒学习的样本复杂度能够与标准（非鲁棒）学习一样，与VC维呈线性关系。

Method: 提出一种简单的不当学习（improper learning）算法：将Breiman的bagging（自助聚合）启发式方法与鲁棒经验风险最小化（RERM）相结合。具体地，算法在O(d*)个独立bootstrap样本上分别计算RERM，然后输出这些假设的多数投票结果，其中d*为对偶VC维。此外，文中还建立了一个下界，证明在通用情况下任何学习器在该oracle模型中都至少需要Ω(d*)次RERM oracle调用。

Result: 证明了VC类的对抗鲁棒可学习性样本复杂度为O(d)，从而将之前的2^{O(d)}上界指数级改进为线性。同时证明了该算法的查询复杂度下界为Ω(d*)，表明使用O(d*)次RERM oracle调用是不可避免的，即所提出的算法在oracle效率上是最优的。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上证明了对抗鲁棒学习在样本复杂度上可以达到与标准学习相同的线性VC维依赖，打破了此前认为对抗鲁棒学习本质上更困难的观点。这也表明经典的bagging方法在对抗鲁棒学习场景中具有重要的理论价值，为设计实用的对抗鲁棒学习算法提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We revisit the problem of learning predictors robust to adversarial examples at test-time. We prove that VC classes are adversarially robustly learnable with sample complexity linear in the VC dimension $d$, providing an exponential improvement over the previous upper bound of Montasser, Hanneke, and Srebro (2019). Remarkably, this result is achieved with a simple improper algorithm that combines the classic heuristic bagging (bootstrap aggregation) of Breiman (1996) with robust empirical risk minimization (RERM). Our algorithm computes RERMs on $O(d^\star)$ independent bootstrap samples and outputs their majority vote, where $d^\star$ denotes the dual VC dimension. We complement this result with a lower bound showing that this is unavoidable: in general, any learner in this oracle model requires $Ω(d^\star)$ calls to an RERM oracle, even when given arbitrarily many training examples.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [207] [Density Estimation on Compact Manifolds under Intrinsic Spectral Block Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12637)
*Olga Klopp,Fedor Noskov*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种内在的谱稀疏模型，用于紧致连通黎曼流形上的非参数密度估计。通过按完整特征空间分组而非逐系数惩罚，构造了基无关、等距不变的块变差空间，建立了其结构、原子和非线性逼近性质，并构建了坐标无关的块收缩估计器与块惩罚指数谱筛，推导了相应的oracle不等式和风险界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统谱方法在黎曼流形上进行密度估计时，依赖于任意选择的Laplace-Beltrami特征基并对系数逐一惩罚，这破坏了基无关性和等距不变性。由于特征空间的简并性（多重性），逐系数处理会导致结果依赖于基的选择，缺乏几何意义。

Method: (1) 将每个完整的特征空间分组，用其谱分量的Hilbert范数衡量稀疏性，定义块变差空间；(2) 建立该空间的结构性质、原子刻画和非线性逼近性质，并与Sobolev、Besov及逐系数谱ℓ1类建立联系；(3) 构造坐标无关的块收缩估计器（block-shrinkage estimator），证明信号依赖的非渐近L²-oracle不等式；(4) 在多项式谱增长假设下进行风险分析，区分由单个高维特征空间驱动的机制与累积谱复杂性驱动的机制；(5) 在谱增长和非退化条件下推导对应的极小极大下界；(6) 发展正的、归一化的、块惩罚指数谱筛用于对数密度估计，推导似然oracle不等式及KL、Hellinger和L²风险界。

Result: 证明了块变差空间的基无关与等距不变性及其与经典函数空间的关系；建立了块收缩估计器的非渐近oracle不等式，将风险分解为谱块数量与多重性两个独立因素，揭示两种风险机制；在匹配条件下给出了极小极大下界，证明多重性依赖是内在的，对球面和旋转群SO(3)有尖锐结论；指数谱筛方法获得了似然oracle不等式及KL、Hellinger和L²风险界。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了尊重几何结构的稀疏密度估计理论，在特征基变换下保持不变性，并揭示了多重性对流形上谱估计风险的固有影响，为球面和SO(3)等对称流形上的统计推断提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: We introduce an intrinsic spectral sparsity model for nonparametric density estimation on compact connected Riemannian manifolds. Instead of penalizing coefficients in an arbitrarily chosen Laplace--Beltrami eigenbasis, we group each complete eigenspace and measure the Hilbert norm of its spectral component. The resulting block-variation space is basis independent and isometry invariant. We establish its structural, atomic, and nonlinear approximation properties and clarify its relation to Sobolev, Besov, and coefficientwise spectral $\ell^1$ classes. We then construct a coordinate-free block-shrinkage estimator and prove a nonasymptotic signal-dependent $L^2$-oracle inequality that adapts to the unknown set of detectable eigenspaces. Under polynomial spectral growth, the risk theory separates the number of spectral blocks from their multiplicities and exhibits two regimes: one driven by a single high-dimensional eigenspace and the other by cumulative spectral complexity. Under matching spectral-growth and nondegeneracy assumptions, corresponding minimax lower bounds show that this multiplicity dependence is intrinsic, with sharp consequences for spheres and the rotation group $SO(3)$. Finally, we develop a positive, normalized, block-penalized exponential spectral sieve for log-densities and derive likelihood oracle inequalities together with expected Kullback--Leibler, Hellinger, and $L^2$ risk bounds. The resulting framework provides a geometry-respecting theory of sparse density estimation that remains invariant under changes of eigenbasis.

</details>


### [208] [Universality of e-detectors for ARL control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12660)
*Aaditya Ramdas*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文建立了e-detector、可选视界不等式与停止时间之间的深刻等价关系：阈值化e-detector产生的停止时间满足可选视界不等式，且反过来，任何满足可选视界不等式的停止时间必然源于阈值化某个e-detector。此外，仅满足ARL控制的停止时间可由"弱"e-detector表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变点检测中，传统上仅关注ARL（平均运行长度）控制。作者希望揭示e-detector除了控制ARL之外更丰富的理论性质——即可选视界不等式——并探索这些性质与停止时间表示之间的对偶关系，从而为设计更稳健的变点检测程序提供理论基石。

Method: 通过数学证明建立e-detector阈值化与可选视界不等式之间的双向等价关系：首先证明任何e-detector阈值化过程满足对任意数据依赖停止时间σ的可选视界不等式；其次通过构造证明，任何满足该不等式的停止时间T必然可表示为某个e-detector的阈值化。对于仅有ARL控制的停止时间，引入"弱"e-detector概念（只需在阈值停止时间上满足不等式）并给出其普适表示。附录部分进一步给出了其他少见变点检测指标的普适表示。

Result: 得到了两个核心定理式的等价关系：(1) e-detector阈值化 ⟺ 可选视界不等式；(2) 弱e-detector阈值化 ⟺ 仅ARL控制。这提供了对变点检测停止时间全类别的精确刻画，并统一了e-detector理论框架。

Conclusion: e-detector不仅是控制ARL的工具，更天然满足可选视界不等式，从而保证在任意固定时刻t的误报概率不超过t/b。该性质的逆命题也成立，说明可选视界不等式本质上完全刻画了e-detector阈值化方法。弱e-detector则为仅追求ARL控制的场景提供了精确表示，形成了完整的理论体系。

Abstract: An e-detector for a pre-change class $\mathcal P$ is a nonnegative process $M$ such that $\mathbb E_P[M_τ] \leq \mathbb E_P[τ]$ for all stopping times $τ$ and all $P \in \mathcal P$. Thresholding e-detectors controls the average run length (ARL): declaring a change at the first time $T_b$ when $M$ crosses $b$ ensures that $\inf_{P \in \mathcal P}\mathbb E_P[T] \geq b$. But e-detectors do substantially more than control the ARL; they also satisfy a \emph{optional-horizon inequality}: \[ P(T_b\leqσ)\leq \mathbb E_P[σ]/b \] for every data-dependent stopping time (monitoring horizon) \(σ\) and $P\in \mathcal P$. In particular, every e-detector-based procedure obeys $P(T\leq t)\leq t/b$ at each fixed $t$, thus avoiding early false alarms. Remarkably, the converse also holds: every stopping time $T$ that satisfies the optional-horizon inequality must in fact arise from thresholding an e-detector. We also derive a universal representation of stopping times that satisfy (only) ARL control. These are represented by \emph{weak} e-detectors, that only require $\mathbb E_P[M_τ] \leq \mathbb E_P[τ]$ to hold at all threshold stopping times $T_b$. Appendices present universal representations for other (less common) change detection metrics.

</details>


### [209] [Sharp proper estimation of fixed-component Gaussian location mixtures in polynomial time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12701)
*Hengzhi He,Guang Cheng*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文解决了一个混合高斯分布估计的开放问题：对于均值位于固定半径球内的至多k个单位协方差高斯混合（无分离或最小权重条件），此前已知最小极大Hellinger风险为√(d/n)∧1，但固定k≥3时多项式时间内达到该最优速率一直未解决。本文通过"矩-纤维范围查找器"、二阶矩子空间、有限个自由索引的Hermite收缩等关键工具，在常维子空间中穷举矩拟合，实现了多项式时间内达到最优Hellinger速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Doss, Wu, Yang和Zhou (2023)已证明混合高斯分布的最小极大Hellinger风险为√(d/n)∧1，并构造了多项式时间但收敛速率较慢（(d/n)^{1/4}）的估计器。对于固定k≥3的情况，在多项式时间内达到最优速率一直是一个开放问题。本文旨在解决这一悬而未决的问题。

Method: 核心创新是"矩-纤维范围查找器"（moment-fiber range finder）。具体方法包括：(1) 利用二阶矩子空间控制投影所遗漏的能量；(2) 估计有限个一自由索引的Hermite收缩（vector-valued contractions），这些收缩能恢复恰好含一个遗漏方向的所有张量分量，达到√(d/n)的精度；(3) 其余每个项至少包含两个遗漏因子，由残余的二阶矩能量控制；(4) 得到的子空间维度仅依赖于k，在该常维空间中进行穷举矩拟合，生成proper mixture，并结合无维矩特征化高斯混合，实现最优Hellinger速率。

Result: 对于任意固定的k，本文实现了在多项式算术时间内达到最优的Hellinger速率（√(d/n)∧1），解决了Doss等人留下的开放问题。

Conclusion: 通过在常维（仅依赖k）子空间中精确的矩拟合，结合精细的Hermite收缩分析和二阶矩能量控制，本文证明了在高维混合高斯估计中，最优收敛速率与多项式计算时间复杂度可以同时实现。

Abstract: We consider a mixture of at most $k$ unit-covariance Gaussians in $\mathbb{R}^d$ whose means belong to a fixed-radius ball, with no separation or minimum-weight condition. Doss, Wu, Yang and Zhou (2023) proved that the minimax Hellinger risk is of order $\sqrt{d/n}\wedge 1$ and constructed a proper polynomial-time estimator with the slower general bound $(d/n)^{1/4}$; obtaining the sharp rate in polynomial time for fixed $k\geq 3$ was left open. We resolve this question. The key device is a moment-fiber range finder. A second-moment subspace controls the energy missed by projection. We then estimate finitely many one-free-index Hermite contractions. These vector-valued contractions recover every tensor component containing exactly one missed direction at the sharp $\sqrt{d/n}$ scale. Every remaining term contains at least two missed factors and is therefore controlled by the residual second-moment energy. The resulting subspace has dimension depending only on $k$. Exhaustive moment fitting in this constant-dimensional space produces a proper mixture and, together with the dimension-free moment characterization of Gaussian mixtures, achieves the optimal Hellinger rate in polynomial arithmetic time for every fixed $k$.

</details>


### [210] [Hyper-V uniform ergodicity of Markov chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12738)
*Austin Brown,Kshitij Khare*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的均匀漂移条件和局部小化条件，从而为马尔可夫链建立更强的加权形式均匀遍历性——称为超V均匀遍历性。该方法绕过了全局小化的需求，却产生比全局小化更强的收敛保证，并建立了最小最大意义下的最优收敛界。通过在Pólya-Gamma和Kolmogorov-Gamma Gibbs采样器上的应用验证了其有效性，并展示了二变量Gibbs采样器的超V均匀遍历性可仅通过不变测度的形式推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统马尔可夫链均匀遍历性的证明通常需要建立全局小化条件（global minorization），这在实践中往往非常难以验证。作者希望开发一种新框架，既能绕开全局小化的困难，又能获得更强的收敛保证，从而为MCMC算法的理论分析提供更实用的工具。

Method: 提出一种新的均匀漂移条件与局部小化条件的组合，这些条件可推导出超V均匀遍历性（hyper-V uniform ergodicity）——即对所有受支配函数V控制的函数，收敛偏差以几何速率衰减且与初始值无关。文中还在最小最大意义下建立了该框架的最优收敛界。

Result: 该方法在Pólya-Gamma和Kolmogorov-Gamma Gibbs采样器上得到了应用验证。此外，还证明了二变量Gibbs采样器的定性超V均匀遍历收敛可以通过不变测度的形式直接推断，从而完全绕过收敛分析。

Conclusion: 新的漂移-小化框架提供了一种替代全局小化的途径，在实践可验证性与收敛保证强度方面均优于传统方法；同时揭示了二变量Gibbs采样器中不变测度结构本身即可决定遍历收敛性质这一深刻联系。

Abstract: We develop a new uniform drift condition and local minorization that implies a stronger weighted form of uniform ergodicity for Markov chains we call hyper-V uniform ergodicity. The convergence guarantees geometric decay of the bias towards the invariant measure independently of the initialization for all functions controlled by a dominating function V. A key advantage of the approach is that it bypasses the need to establish a global minorization condition, which is often substantially more difficult to verify in practice, while yielding stronger convergence guarantees than global minorization. Optimal convergence bounds in a minimax sense of the framework are established. The utility of the framework is demonstrated through applications to the P'olya-Gamma and Kolmogorov-Gamma Gibbs samplers. We also show qualitative hyper-V uniform ergodicity convergence for two-variable Gibbs samplers can be inferred by the form of the invariant measure, bypassing convergence analysis entirely.

</details>


### [211] [Weighted Nuclear Elastic Net Estimation of (Near-) Low-Rank Drift Matrices in Ornstein-Uhlenbeck Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12838)
*Dmytro Marushkevych,Francisco Pina,Mark Podolskij*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究连续观测的高维Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程中漂移矩阵的估计问题，针对精确或近似低秩情形提出了一种加权核弹性网估计器（Weighted Nuclear Elastic Net Estimator），结合岭正则化与经验似然几何中的核范数惩罚。在一般的可对角化谱框架下建立了相对任意低秩比较矩阵的oracle不等式，并在对称半正定精确低秩模型下验证了经验曲率条件，实现平方Frobenius误差为O(rd log(T)/T)的估计界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程中精确低秩漂移会导致非稳定方向和非遍历机制，使得经验协方差矩阵病态，传统估计方法失效。现有方法难以同时处理低秩结构约束和高维病态协方差带来的挑战，亟需一种能够在非遍历、病态协方差条件下有效估计漂移矩阵的方法。

Method: 提出加权核弹性网估计器，将岭正则化与核范数惩罚结合，并在经验似然几何中表示惩罚项。利用自归一化鞅论证控制随机项，通过选择适当的调整参数建立oracle不等式。在对称半正定精确低秩模型中，验证经验曲率条件，将加权界转化为Frobenius范数界。

Result: 在一般的可对角化谱框架下建立了相对任意低秩比较矩阵的oracle不等式；对近似低秩漂移，近似误差通过加权正则化经验协方差后的奇异值衰减自然度量。在对称半正定精确低秩模型下，所得估计器在适当的调整参数选择下，平方Frobenius误差达到O(rd log(T)/T)的量级（高概率），满足标准秩r矩阵估计的rd/T缩放（含对数因子）。

Conclusion: 该研究为高维连续时间随机过程的低秩漂移估计提供了理论保证，通过加权核弹性网方法有效克服了非遍历机制下的病态协方差问题，并建立了与标准矩阵估计理论相匹配的最优（对数值因子）误差界。

Abstract: We study estimation of the drift matrix in a continuously observed high-dimensional Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process when the drift is exactly or approximately low rank. In this setting, exact low rank induces non-stable directions and hence a non-ergodic regime, resulting in a poorly conditioned empirical covariance matrix. To address this difficulty, we introduce a Weighted Nuclear Elastic Net Estimator that combines ridge regularization with a nuclear-norm penalty expressed in the empirical likelihood geometry.
  Under a general diagonalizable spectral framework, we establish oracle inequalities relative to arbitrary low-rank comparison matrices. For near low-rank drifts, the approximation error is naturally measured through the singular-value decay of the drift after weighting by the regularized empirical covariance. The stochastic term is controlled by self-normalized martingale arguments under appropriate choice of the tuning parameter.
  For a symmetric positive-semidefinite exact low-rank model, we verify the empirical-curvature condition required to translate the weighted bound into a Frobenius-norm bound. With an appropriate choice of tuning parameters, the resulting estimator satisfies, up to a logarithmic factor, the standard rank-$r$ matrix-estimation scaling $r d/T$: specifically, its squared Frobenius error is of order $r d\log(T)/T$ with high probability, under an explicit dimension-horizon condition.

</details>


### [212] [Foundations of Independent Component Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13229)
*Patrick Forré*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文以测度论概率论为背景，系统建立了线性独立成分分析（ICA）模型的数学基础，涵盖特征函数理论、不同强度假设下ICA模型的可辨识性结果，并展示了在线等变梯度下降ICA算法用于从数据中恢复独立源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICA模型在盲源分离等领域有广泛应用，但其严格的数学基础（特别是基于特征函数理论和可辨识性证明）在标准文献中较为分散。作者希望提供一个自包含、面向具有测度论概率背景读者的严格数学论述，系统地建立ICA的理论基础。

Method: 本文首先发展R^d上概率测度特征函数的理论，包括其解析性以及特征函数如何决定和刻画分布。在此基础上，针对源变量的假设从非恒定、到非高斯、再到无高斯分量的逐步强化，分别建立了不同层次的ICA可辨识性结果。在最严格的假设下，证明了独立源即使在存在加性高斯噪声的情况下，也可在平移、置换、缩放和符号意义上被辨识。最后给出在线等变梯度下降ICA算法，用于标准完整无噪非高斯ICA设置下恢复独立源。

Result: 在最严格的假设（高斯自由的独立源）下，ICA模型中的独立源可在平移、置换、缩放和符号变换下被唯一辨识，且此结论在存在加性高斯噪声时依然成立。同时给出了在线等变梯度下降ICA算法的收敛恢复方案。

Conclusion: 本文为ICA模型提供了自包含、严格的数学基础，明确了不同假设强度下可辨识性的边界，并将理论结果与实用的在线ICA算法联系起来，兼具理论严谨性与实践指导意义。

Abstract: We present the mathematical foundations of linear independent component analysis (ICA) models based on standard literature in a self-contained note. It is aimed at readers with a background in measure-theoretic probability theory. We first develop the theory of the characteristic functions of probability measures on $\mathbb{R}^d$, including their analyticity and the way in which they determine and characterise the distributions. We then focus on several identifiability results of ICA models with successively strengthened assumptions on the sources: from merely non-constant, to non-Gaussian, to Gaussian-free independent sources. Under the strictest assumptions, we show that the independent sources are identifiable up to translation, permutation, scales and signs, and this even in the presence of additive Gaussian noise. Furthermore, we present the online equivariant gradient descent ICA algorithm for recovering the independent sources from data, in the standard complete noiseless non-Gaussian ICA setting.

</details>


### [213] [On Bridging Mixture Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13383)
*Pierre Del Moral,Ajay Jasra,Ke Zhao*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究两个混合概率测度之间的桥接问题。给定两个分量分布之间的马尔可夫核，作者提出了一种通用机制来从混合分布中生成样本到另一个混合分布，并证明了该方法的熵最优性。针对高斯混合和Schrödinger桥的具体情况，作者基于ε-协方差膨胀方法证明了精确桥与近似桥之间的2-Wasserstein连续性界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合概率测度之间的桥接在许多应用中都具重要性，但精确的混合成分与马尔可夫核通常不可得。作者旨在提供一种理论上最优且可实际估计的桥接方法，并给出可靠的误差界分析。

Method: 作者首先基于参考状态空间与扩展状态空间建立了混合分布间桥接的一般框架并证明其熵最优性。随后针对高斯混合与Schrödinger桥的特定情形，采用ε-协方差膨胀来近似，并通过对扰动Riccati映射进行新颖的连续性分析，推导出精确桥与近似桥之间的2-Wasserstein连续性界。对于参数估计，分别使用期望最大化算法估计高斯混合参数，以及经验估计器估计高斯参数和Monge映射。

Result: 对于高斯混合情形，当参数通过EM算法估计时，在假设条件下且概率至少为1-10N^{-1}，精确桥与近似桥之间2-Wasserstein距离的上界为O([(d log N / N)^{1/2}{1+(d log N / N)^{1/2}(ε^{-2}+1)}+ε^2])；对于另外两种情形（单高斯和经验估计器），在期望意义下界为O(d{(1+ε^{-2})/(1+N)+ε^2})，其中d为高斯维数，N为经验样本数。数值实验验证了这些理论界的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为混合概率分布间的桥接提供了理论最优且可计算的框架，并给出了具有明确收敛速率（依赖于维数d、样本数N和协方差膨胀参数ε）的误差界，为实际应用提供了理论保障。

Abstract: In this article we consider bridging between two mixture probability measures. In particular, given access to a Markov kernel between two component distributions, we provide a general mechanism to generate samples from one mixture to the other. Associated to a given reference and extended state space, we prove entropic optimality of this approach. In order to use this idea one needs to know the underlying mixtures and the Markov kernel, which is seldom available, and so we consider the case of Gaussian mixtures and Schrödinger Bridges. We prove a general $2-$Wasserstein continuity bound between the exact bridge and one that is approximated, based on $ε-$covariance inflation, and these rely on a novel continuity analysis of perturbed Riccati maps. We apply our results in the context of bridging mixtures of Gaussians, single Gaussians and empirical estimators of the Gaussian parameters and the Monge map. For mixtures of Gaussians, when the parameters are estimated using the Expectation-Maxmization algorithm, the upper-bound on the $2-$Wasserstein distance between the true and approximated bridges is, under assumptions and with probability at least $1-10N^{-1}$, $\mathcal{O}\big(\big[\big(\tfrac{d\log N}{N}\big)^{1/2}\left\{1+\big(\tfrac{d\log N}{N}\right)^{1/2}(ε^{-2}+1)\big\}+ε^2\big]\big) $
and for the other two cases, in expectation, $\mathcal{O}\left(d\left\{\tfrac{1+ε^{-2}}{1+N}+ε^2\right\}\right)$, where $d,N\in\mathbb{N}$ is the dimension of the Gaussian and the number of empirical samples respectively. We also investigate our bounds numerically.

</details>


### [214] [On the Structural Limits of Machine Learning Decision Systems: An Information-Theoretic, Interaction-Based, and Stochastic-Dynamical Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13510)
*Nestor R. Barraza,Gabriel Pena*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文从信息论和交互式建模的视角审视数据驱动决策系统的内在极限，通过Fano界和Cramér-Rao不等式分别分析了分类误差和参数估计精度的下限，指出这些限制取决于底层数据生成模型而非算法复杂度。文章还讨论了独立性、遍历性和分布稳定性等隐含假设对推断有效性的影响，并回顾了马尔可夫随机场等交互建模框架，最终将包括LLM智能体架构在内的决策系统描述为反馈驱动的随机过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习算法的评估通常关注预测精度和计算效率，但其可达到的性能本质上受限于数据生成过程的结构属性。作者希望从信息论和交互式视角系统性地刻画这些内在边界，以揭示算法学习的根本约束，并强调构建合适的数据模型是扩展预测能力的先决条件。

Method: 采用理论分析的方法：(1)通过Fano型界分析分类的最小可达误差，通过Cramér-Rao不等式分析参数估计的精度极限；(2)讨论独立性、遍历性和分布稳定性等隐含假设对推断有效性的影响；(3)基于交互式建模原则，回顾马尔可夫随机场和基于势函数的表示框架用于编码依赖机制；(4)将决策系统（包括LLM集成智能体架构）建模为反馈驱动的随机过程。

Result: 揭示了机器学习性能的极限由底层数据生成模型决定而非仅由算法复杂度决定；指出了隐含统计假设对推断有效性的关键影响；论证了马尔可夫随机场等交互式建模框架在编码依赖机制中的价值；将LLM集成智能体等决策系统形式化为反馈驱动随机过程，可能涌现宏观行为。

Conclusion: 算法学习应被置于模型所施加的信息论极限之内来理解；拥有与数据生成机制相匹配的充分模型是扩展预测能力的先决条件。该视角将信息论边界、交互式建模与新型决策系统架构联系起来，为机器学习提供更基础性的理论定位。

Abstract: Machine learning procedures are commonly evaluated in terms of predictive accuracy and computational efficiency. However, their achievable performance is fundamentally constrained by structural properties of the underlying data-generating process, which are formalized in terms of informational bounds. In this work we examine intrinsic limits of data-driven decision systems from an information-theoretic and interaction-based perspective. We analyze minimal achievable error in classification through Fano-type bounds and precision limits in parametric estimation via the Cramér-Rao inequality, emphasizing that such limits depend on the underlying model rather than on algorithmic sophistication alone. We further discuss how implicit assumptions, such as independence, ergodicity, and distributional stability, affect the validity of inferential procedures. Building on interaction-based modeling principles, we review typical frameworks such as Markov Random Fields and potential based representations for encoding dependence mechanisms. We also describe decision systems, including LLM-integrated agent architectures, as feedback-driven stochastic processes where state-dependent dynamics may induce emergent macroscopic behavior. This perspective highlights the importance of having adequate models for the data as a prerequi- site for expanding predictive capability, and situates algorithmic learning within the informational limits imposed by the models.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [215] [LoKiFormer: Locality-aware Attention with Decoupled Knowledge Memory for Efficient Large Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12419)
*Qiuwu Chen,Zimo Liu,Yuchen Li,Ying Sun,Yifan Zhang,Zhijie Qiu,Zeng You,Ryan Dong,Simeng Ma,Yaofo Chen,Mingkui Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoKiFormer架构，通过局部融合注意力（LFA）和知识记忆模块（KMM）分别解决LLM预训练中自注意力缺乏局部归纳偏置以及MoE将知识存储与计算路径隐式耦合的问题，使预训练收敛速度提升1.33倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM架构预训练效率低，原因有二：一是自注意力缺乏显式的局部归纳偏置，导致对序列内部局部信息进行冗余建模；二是混合专家（MoE）将知识存储与计算路径隐式耦合，阻碍了对序列外部全局知识的灵活访问。

Method: LoKiFormer在标准decoder上新增两个模块：1）LFA（局部融合注意力），在注意力机制中融入卷积融合，显式捕获局部模式，使注意力基于更具信息量的表征进行计算；2）KMM（知识记忆模块），引入参数化的键值记忆，将全局知识显式存储在可寻址的槽位中，将存储与计算解耦，支持直接的知识检索。

Result: 实验结果表明，LoKiFormer在预训练阶段的收敛速度比基线模型快1.33倍，验证了其在信息整合效率与有效性方面的优越性。

Conclusion: LoKiFormer通过解耦局部信息建模与全局知识存储，实现了更高效的LLM预训练，证明了显式地处理局部性与知识存储两个层面的结构设计对提升架构效率的必要性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable breakthroughs across various applications. However, their architectures remain inefficient in pretraining due to two main limitations: (i) self-attention lacks an explicit inductive bias for locality, leading to redundant modeling of sequence-internal local information; (ii) mixture-of-experts (MoE) implicitly couples knowledge storage with computational pathways, hindering flexible access to sequence-external global knowledge. To overcome these limitations, we propose LoKiFormer, a novel LLM architecture that augments the standard decoder with two dedicated modules: 1) Local Fusion Attention (LFA), which incorporates a convolutional fusion to attention, explicitly capturing local patterns and allowing the attention to operate on more informative representations; 2) Knowledge Memory Module (KMM), which introduces a parametric key-value memory that explicitly stores global knowledge in addressable slots, decoupling storage from computation and enabling direct knowledge retrieval. Together, these modules enable LoKiFormer to achieve more efficient and effective integration of information at both levels. Experimental results show that LoKiFormer converges 1.33x faster in pre-training than baseline models, underscoring its superiority over existing LLM architectures.

</details>


### [216] [Which Site, and When: A Free-Satellite-Data Test of Himalayan Glacial Lake Bursts, Landslides, and Ice Floods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12422)
*Matthew Kahn,Milan Arjel,Nirmala Adhikari,Mingmar Sherpa,James Pope*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用免费卫星数据（雷达干涉测量与卫星天气数据）针对尼泊尔喜马拉雅地区的冰湖溃决洪水、降雨触发滑坡及冰川小洪水三种灾害，构建了预测模型。通过空间交叉验证（保留整块地图瓦片），发现前期气象条件能较好预测触发时机（ROC 0.73-0.83），而地形对易发性排序仅在部分情况下有效（匹配同类未溃决地点后为0.76/0.71/0.54）。五种深度学习模型未显著超越梯度提升基线，湖泊灾害中简单三规则决策树甚至胜出。最终提供尼泊尔排序监测清单作为优先排序工具而非预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有可行性研究已证明雷达干涉测量可识别冰湖溃决风险（变形指示脆弱湖，天气指示风险时段），但未提出预测模型。作者旨在填补该空白，仅凭免费数据构建能够预测"哪个地点易发、何时触发"的模型，并严格检验其真实预测能力。

Method: 对三种灾害（大型冰碛坝/冰坝溃决、降雨触发滑坡、冰川池塘小洪水）分别提出两个独立问题（易发性与触发时机）。利用589个已标注溃决事件（HMAGLOFDB）和数千个目录滑坡，将每个事件与相似但未失稳的地点匹配。采用空间交叉验证（保留整块地图瓦片）防止模型通过识别训练邻域而"作弊"，并与强简单基线对比。测试五种深度学习模型及一个梯度提升基线，外加一个三规则决策树。

Result: 前期天气触发时机预测：大型溃决ROC 0.73、滑坡0.83、小洪水0.82。地形易发性朴素评分为约0.9，但匹配同类邻近地点后降至0.76、0.71、0.54（后者无异于随机）。溃决信号在单一区域内仍成立（尼泊尔境内0.89）。深度学习模型未决定性胜过梯度提升基线；滑坡上有微弱优势但统计上不足以确认；湖泊灾害中基线模型胜出，三规则决策树（崎岖度+季风降雨）重现其结果。

Conclusion: 免费卫星数据足以对冰湖溃决和滑坡风险提供有意义的排序与触发预警，但需要诚实的方法论（如匹配对照与空间交叉验证）以避免虚高精度。该研究交付尼泊尔风险排序观察清单作为优先级排序工具而非预测，并明确指出免费数据的能力边界。

Abstract: Two free satellite signals carry real information about glacial-lake outburst risk in the Nepal Himalaya: radar interferometry sees a moraine dam slowly sagging, and satellite weather marks the weeks when a primed lake is under stress. A companion feasibility study found that deformation indicates which lake is destabilizing and weather indicates when it is at risk, but proposed no predictive model. To address this gap, we propose and evaluate models that predict which site is susceptible and when a trigger arrives. We test three related hazards on free data alone: large moraine- and ice-dammed bursts, rainfall-triggered landslides, and smaller floods from ponds on and around a glacier. Each hazard gets two questions, never blended. Using 589 dated outbursts from HMAGLOFDB and several thousand catalogued landslides, we match each event against similar but unfailed sites, and hold every model to a strong simple baseline under spatial cross-validation that withholds whole map tiles, so no model succeeds by recognising a trained-on neighbourhood. Antecedent weather times the trigger at ROC 0.73 for big bursts, 0.83 for landslides, and 0.82 for small floods. Terrain ranks susceptibility only in part: scored naively it appears near 0.9, largely because catalogued failures cluster in wetter ranges; matched against comparable nearby sites the honest figures are 0.76, 0.71, and 0.54 (no better than chance). The burst signal holds within single regions, reaching 0.89 in Nepal alone. Five deep-learning models do not decisively beat a simple gradient-boosted baseline. Three score marginally higher on landslides, a hint too small to confirm. For the lake hazards the baseline wins outright, reproduced by a three-rule decision tree on ruggedness and monsoon rainfall. We close with a ranked Nepal watchlist, a prioritisation aid, not a prediction, and note where free data reaches its limits.

</details>


### [217] [MARCH: Scaling Recurrent Memory with Content-Routed State Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12435)
*Ming Zhang,Kaisen Yang,Shu Yu,Ermo Hua,Ning Ding,Xia Hu,Bowen Zhou,Chaochao Lu,Youbang Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MARCH（Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History）架构，通过周期性缓存循环状态检查点作为"状态锚点"，并为每个锚点关联内容条件化的锚点键，使得状态空间模型能够超越固定尺寸限制，在保持长序列计算效率的同时显著增强长上下文检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的长上下文检索能力依赖随上下文增长的token级记忆，但训练时计算复杂度呈二次方增长，推理时KV缓存线性增长。循环替代方案虽有高效解码，但固定尺寸状态难以保留早期信息，在召回密集型任务上表现不佳。研究者希望设计一种既能保持循环模型解码效率，又能突破固定状态尺寸限制的架构。

Method: MARCH周期性缓存累积的循环状态检查点作为状态锚点，每个锚点关联一个紧凑的内容条件化锚点键。它维护一个可随上下文长度增长的内存银行，提供历史分辨率与存储成本之间的可控权衡。每个token生成锚点查询，对所有因果可用的状态锚点进行注意力聚合，输出沿当前状态对所有历史锚点进行注意力式聚合。

Result: 经过标准预训练后，MARCH在常识推理、LongBench和上下文检索任务上一致优于多种线性注意力变体，证明内容路由状态缓存能显著增强循环模型的长期记忆能力。

Conclusion: MARCH通过内容路由的状态缓存机制，在保留循环模型原生高效计算路径的同时，显著强化了其长程记忆能力，为状态空间模型在长上下文任务上提供了一种计算高效的扩展方案。

Abstract: Transformers owe much of their strong long-context retrieval capability to a token-level memory that grows with context length. This flexibility, however, incurs a quadratic computation complexity during training and a key--value cache that grows linearly during autoregressive inference. Recurrent alternatives offer efficient decoding by compressing the entire history into a fixed-size state, but often underperform on recall-intensive tasks since earlier associations usually get overwritten by subsequent updates, and only the most recent contextual information is retained. In this paper, we introduce Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History (MARCH), a network architecture that effectively scales state-space models beyond a fixed-size dimension, while maintaining computational efficiency over long-sequences. MARCH periodically caches cumulative recurrent-state checkpoints as state anchors and associates each anchor with a compact, content-conditioned anchor key. This lets MARCH maintain a memory bank, which can grow as context length increases, providing a controllable trade-off between historical resolution and memory cost. At each token, MARCH produces an anchor query to attend all causally available state anchors, and the output is calculated as an attention-style aggregation over all historical anchors along the current state. We show that after standard pretraining, MARCH consistently outperforms multiple linear attention variants across commonsense reasoning, LongBench, and in-context retrieval. These results demonstrate that content-routed state caching substantially strengthens recurrent long-range memory while preserving its native computation path.

</details>


### [218] [Multi-AUV Ad-hoc network-based Target Tracking: A Value Gradient Guidance Multi-Agent Diffusion Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12436)
*Jiaao Ma,Chuan Lin,Guangjie Han,Shengchao Zhu,Qian Zhu,Ying Liu,Zhenyu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VGG-MADiffRL（值梯度引导的多智能体扩散强化学习算法）和MDCA（扩散式分层控制架构），用于多AUV自组网在受限声学通信、动态拓扑和海洋扰动下的协同目标跟踪。MDCA为三层闭环控制框架，VGG-MADiffRL在扩散策略基础上引入值梯度引导反向去噪过程生成动作，并采用双子网络联合优化与软目标更新抑制过估计和训练震荡，实验验证其在收敛速度、跟踪精度和训练稳定性上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多AUV自组网协同跟踪机动目标面临受限声通信、动态拓扑和海洋扰动等挑战。虽然多智能体强化学习（MARL）可通过集中式训练实现分布式协调，但现有方法存在高维联合状态-动作建模和噪声敏感策略生成的问题，导致训练不稳定和跟踪性能退化。

Method: 首先基于水下任务特性建模声呐探测机制和洋流扰动，将多AUV协同跟踪形式化为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）。提出MDCA三层闭环控制架构：全局智能控制层、本地在线训练层和物理动作执行层，实现任务分配、局部决策与执行反馈的协同优化。在本地在线训练层中，VGG-MADiffRL基于扩散策略，在反向去噪过程中引入值梯度来引导动作生成朝向更高期望回报，并采用双子价值网络联合优化和软目标更新来抑制过估计与训练振荡。

Result: 实验结果表明，VGG-MADiffRL在协同跟踪场景中持续实现更快的收敛速度、更高的跟踪精度和更平滑的训练动态，验证了其在动态水下环境中的有效性和实际工程价值。

Conclusion: VGG-MADiffRL与MDCA框架有效解决了多AUV自组网协同跟踪中高维状态-动作建模和噪声敏感策略生成的问题，通过值梯度引导的扩散策略和分层控制架构，实现了稳定训练和高质量跟踪，具备实际工程应用潜力。

Abstract: Multi-AUV ad-hoc network-based target tracking requires networked autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) to cooperatively track maneuvering targets under constrained acoustic communication, dynamic topology, and uncertain ocean disturbances. Although multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) enables decentralized coordination through centralized training, existing methods suffer from high-dimensional joint state-action modeling, noise-sensitive policy generation, leading to unstable training and degraded tracking. To address these issues, we propose VGG-MADiffRL, a value-gradient-guided multi-agent diffusion RL algorithm, and MDCA, a diffusion?based hierarchical control architecture. Leveraging underwater mission characteristics, we model sonar detection mechanisms and ocean current disturbances, formulating cooperative tracking for multi-AUV ad-hoc networks as an MDP. The proposed MDCA constitutes a three-tier closed-loop control framework: a global intelligent control layer, a local online training layer, and a physical action execution layer. This structure enables synergistic optimization across task allocation, local decision processes, and execution feedback. Within MDCA, the local online training layer is the policy learning framework; VGG-MADiffRL builds on diffusion policies and incorporates value gradients to guide action generation in the reverse denoising process, steering the generated actions towards higher expected returns. It employs twin value networks with joint optimization and soft target updates to mitigate overestimation and training oscillations, promoting more stable convergence. Experimental results show that VGG-MADiffRL consistently achieves faster convergence, higher tracking accuracy, and smoother training dynamics in cooperative tracking scenarios, validating its effectiveness and practical engineering value in dynamic underwater settings.

</details>


### [219] [Unifying Generative Models with Path Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12438)
*Ramon Winterhalder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将生成建模表述为路径积分框架，其中基于流、扩散、变分和对抗的生成模型均被统一为同一主作用量下的不同评估原理。通过MSRJD形式将自由概率流与相互作用概率流分离，并引入图论微扰论。该展开为确定性采样器提供了单圈修正，无需额外随机采样成本；不完美的学习分数被视为插入项，导出了响应加权的分数匹配目标；对称等变漂移设计被转化为带EFT功率计数的算子展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成模型（流模型、扩散模型、变分模型、对抗模型）各自基于不同的原理和框架，缺乏统一的数学基础。作者希望建立一个统一的生成建模理论框架，使得不同模型能够在一个主作用量下互相联系，并借助统计场论的成熟工具（路径积分、微扰论）来分析和改进生成模型的性能。

Method: 将生成建模表述为路径积分，借助MSRJD（Martin-Siggia-Rose-Janssen-de-Dominicis）形式将自由概率流与相互作用概率流分离，从而应用图论微扰展开。具体包括：(1) 单圈修正项用于改进确定性采样器；(2) 将不完美学习的分数作为插入项，推导响应加权分数匹配目标；(3) 将对称等变漂移设计转化为算子展开并引入EFT（有效场论）功率计数。

Result: 在可解的与非线性漂移问题上验证了单圈修正的有效性，将53%的树级误差降低至1.6%，且不增加随机采样成本。

Conclusion: 该工作为生成模型建立了统一的场论视角，使得不同生成模型范式可在统一框架下理解，并提供了微扰修正、改进分数学习目标以及等变设计等实际工具，展示了理论框架对生成模型实践的指导价值。

Abstract: We formulate generative modeling as a path integral in which flow-based, diffusion-based, variational, and adversarial models arise as different evaluation principles for a single master action. Its Martin-Siggia-Rose-Janssen-de~Dominicis (MSRJD) form separates free from interacting probability flows and opens them to diagrammatic perturbation theory. The expansion yields a one-loop correction to deterministic samplers at no stochastic-sampling cost, which we validate on solvable and nonlinear drifts, where it reduces a 53 % tree-level error to 1.6 %. Imperfect learned scores enter as insertions and yield a response-weighted score-matching objective, and symmetry-equivariant drift design becomes an operator expansion with EFT power counting.

</details>


### [220] [Dual Spatial-Temporal Attribution: Architecture-Aligned Post-Hoc Explainability for Recurrent Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12441)
*Iyad Assaad Nekka,Hamida Seba,Khaled Walid Hidouci,Karima Amrouche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出X-AddGraph，一个针对动态图边级异常检测模型AddGraph的严格事后可解释性框架。它通过双时空归因机制（空间梯度归因、短期时间注意力、长期时间梯度回滚）为检测结果提供解释，同时保持检测性能完全不变（Delta AUC=0）。在UCI Message基准上验证了长期归因能识别出比随机选择携带更多反事实信号的历史快照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习异常检测器在动态图上已达到强准确率，但缺乏可解释性——当边缘被标记为异常时，分析者只收到分数而没有任何理由。在需要可审计、可信任自动决策的合作式监管信息系统中，这种不透明性不可接受。而AddGraph作为动态图边级异常检测的基础性GCN+GRU框架，从未配备任何形式的可解释性工具。

Method: 提出严格事后（post-hoc）可解释性框架X-AddGraph，基于双时空归因（DSTA）机制，包含三个与AddGraph架构模块对齐的组件：1）基于梯度的相关性归因作用于当前邻接结构（空间维度）；2）直接读取推理期间已计算的上下文注意力权重（短期时间维度，零额外成本）；3）通过循环隐藏状态进行梯度回滚（长期时间维度）。检测器保持冻结状态，因此检测性能完全保持不变。

Result: 在UCI Message基准上，训练好的AddGraph基线实现了平均每快照AUC 0.8705，超过原始发表结果；X-AddGraph完全复现每个分数同时添加解释。在四种边缘群体（高置信真阳性、低置信真阳性、假阳性、随机样本）中，长期归因识别出的历史快照携带的反事实信号显著高于随机选择（0.127 vs 0.074），这是任何空间盲解释器无法提供的。

Conclusion: X-AddGraph证明可以在不牺牲任何检测性能的前提下为冻结的动态图异常检测模型提供多维度可解释性，并验证了长期时间归因在提供有价值反事实信息方面的独特优势。

Abstract: Deep learning detectors for anomalies in dynamic graphs have reached strong accuracy, yet they remain opaque: when an edge is flagged, the analyst receives a score but no reason. This opacity is untenable in the cooperative, regulated information systems where such detectors are deployed, where automated decisions must be auditable and trustworthy. We address this gap for AddGraph, the foundational GCN+GRU framework for edge-level anomaly detection in dynamic graphs, which to our knowledge has never been equipped with any form of explainability. We present a strictly post-hoc explainability framework, X-AddGraph, built on a Dual Spatial-Temporal Attribution (DSTA) mechanism whose three components are each aligned with one of AddGraph's architectural modules: a gradient-based relevance attribution over the current adjacency structure (spatial), a direct reading of the contextual attention weights already computed during inference (short-term temporal, at zero additional cost), and a gradient rollback through the recurrent hidden states (long-term temporal). Because the detector is frozen, detection performance is preserved exactly (Delta AUC = 0, verified empirically to ten decimal places). On the UCI Message benchmark, our trained AddGraph baseline reaches an average per-snapshot AUC of 0.8705, exceeding the originally published result; X-AddGraph reproduces every score identically while adding explanations where none existed. Evaluated across four edge populations - confident true positives, low-confidence true positives, false positives, and random samples - the long-term attribution identifies historical snapshots carrying significantly more counterfactual signal than random selection (0.127 vs. 0.074), a capability that no spatially-blind explainer can provide. We release our implementation for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [221] [Personalized Scorer Modeling: A Learning-Based Framework for Deriving Robust Sleep Stage Labels from Multiple Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12446)
*Seyyed Ali Hoseini,Javad Baseri,Hamid Saadatfar,Edris Hoseini Gol,AmirHossein Eshghi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于学习的睡眠分期标注构建方法（LBH），利用多评分者数据集和混淆矩阵建模每个评分者的分期行为，将不同专家的标注概率聚合生成更可靠的参考睡眠图。在DOD-H和DOD-O数据集上，随机森林结合EEG+EMG特征取得最佳性能（约86%的准确率、精确率和F1值），优于数据集自带标注和最佳评分者标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动睡眠分期研究通常以单一参考睡眠图为评价标准，但已知不同评分者之间存在显著的标注变异性（inter-scorer variability）。这一单一参考标注可能不准确甚至不公平，因此需要探索利用多评分者数据构建更可靠的参考标注，以提高自动分期模型评估的可信度。

Method: 使用公开的DOD-H和DOD-O数据集，从EEG（C3-M2）和下巴EMG信号中提取30秒时间窗的30个特征（每个模态），共60个特征。提出学习型睡眠图（LBH）：利用机器学习模型（随机森林、支持向量机、多层感知机）为每个评分者构建混淆矩阵，经列归一化后估计在给定评分者标注条件下各真实睡眠阶段的概率，跨评分者聚合这些概率并为每个epoch分配最终标签。在EEG-only和EEG+EMG两种设置下评估，并与数据集自带睡眠图（DH）和最佳评分者睡眠图（BSH）进行对比。

Result: LBH在两种数据集和所有分类器设置下均持续提升整体性能。最佳结果由随机森林结合EEG+EMG取得：DOD-H上准确率86.07%、精确率85.46%、F1值85.29%；DOD-O上准确率86.04%、精确率85.21%、F1值84.70%。LBH一致优于DH和BSH。

Conclusion: 个性化评分者建模能够在保留各专家信息的同时改进参考睡眠图的构建，为多评分者标注数据在自动睡眠分期评估中的使用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Sleep stage classification is important for the diagnosis and management of sleep disorders, yet most automatic staging studies evaluate models against a single reference hypnogram despite known inter-scorer variability. This study investigates whether multi-scored datasets can be used to construct more reliable reference labels from the collective behavior of multiple experts. We use the publicly available DOD-H and DOD-O datasets. EEG (C3-M2) and chin EMG signals were segmented into 30-s epochs, and 30 features were extracted from each modality, yielding 60 features for EEG+EMG. We propose a learning-based hypnogram (LBH) that models the stage-specific behavior of each scorer using confusion matrices derived from machine-learning models. After column normalization, these matrices estimate the probability of each true sleep stage given each scorer's label; probabilities are aggregated across scorers to assign the final label for each epoch. LBH was evaluated with random forest, support vector machine, and multilayer perceptron classifiers under EEG-only and EEG+EMG settings, and compared with the dataset hypnogram (DH) and best-scorer hypnogram (BSH). LBH consistently improved overall performance. The best results were obtained with random forest and EEG+EMG, reaching 86.07% accuracy, 85.46% precision, and 85.29% F1-score on DOD-H, and 86.04% accuracy, 85.21% precision, and 84.70% F1-score on DOD-O. These findings suggest that personalized scorer modeling can improve reference hypnogram construction without discarding information from individual experts.

</details>


### [222] [Geometric and Behavioral Stratification in Transformer Residual Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12447)
*Nelson Guda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现训练后的Transformer模型中，预测方向（prediction direction）作为一个内容定义的、特权化锚点，其与残差流中其他方向在几何和行为上呈现分层结构。该锚点在所有18个测试模型中普遍成立，预测近端区域高度结构化且因果关键，而远端区域平坦但在因果和时间上承担承载作用，且行为由方向而非大小驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的研究观察到训练后的Transformer会形成特权基（privileged bases），即某些坐标轴统计特性不同于残差流其余部分。然而这些坐标轴选择了何种方向、由什么定义、如何组织计算，尚不清楚。本文旨在识别并刻画这种特权基选取的具体方向，并解释高维计算如何与线性读出（linear readout）共存。

Method: 作者将模型的非嵌入方向（unembedding direction）——即当前预测token对应的读出方向——定义为"内容定义的锚点"。以该锚点为参照，分析残差流个方向的方差分布、几何结构、聚类特性以及因果扰动效应。测试范围涵盖18个模型（稠密和MoE架构，7B-120B参数，基座和指令微调），并对比了方差主成分分析与锚点分析在揭示组织结构上的差异。

Result: 锚点揭示出陡峭的几何梯度：预测近端区域高度结构化、聚类相关提示，远端区域平坦且对抗性区分提示组。预测接口窄但功能决定性强——破坏最接近预测的方差方向导致立即发散和任务帧迁移，破坏次近方向则延迟发散但保留帧结构。远端方向每个方向弱对齐于读出，但整体因果和时间上负载重要。方差分析只能部分恢复该组织，且缺口随提示异质性增大。

Conclusion: 预测方向是被优先选中的锚点，区别于以往描述的其他坐标轴。研究给出了高维计算与线性读出共存的几何解释：窄而结构化强的预测接口承担读出的决定性功能，广阔的远端区域在整体上为计算提供因果和时间承载，行为由方向而非幅度驱动。

Abstract: Trained transformer models develop privileged bases: coordinate axes whose statistics differ from the rest of the residual stream. But what kind of direction does such a basis select? We investigate the prediction direction, the unembedding direction of the token a model currently predicts, and find that it functions as a content-defined privileged anchor. Measured with respect to this anchor, residual-stream variation is geometrically and behaviorally stratified by proximity to the prediction.
  The stratification holds in all eighteen models tested (dense and mixture-of-experts, 7B-120B, base and instruction-tuned). A narrow, scale-invariant prediction interface concentrates readout-relevant structure, while the vast prediction-distal complement expands with model scale. Because the prediction direction sits nearly orthogonal to the principal variance axes, variance-based analyses recover this organization only partly, and the shortfall grows with prompt heterogeneity.
  Anchoring reveals a steep geometric gradient: prediction-proximal regions are highly structured and cluster related prompts, while the complement is flatter and anti-discriminates among prompt groups. The interface is a narrow slice but functionally decisive. Disrupting the variance directions closest to the prediction causes immediate divergence and frequent task-frame shifts; disrupting the next level down delays divergence and preserves framing. The complement is weakly readout-aligned per direction yet causally and temporally load-bearing, and behavior is driven by direction rather than magnitude.
  These results establish the prediction direction as a privileged anchor distinct from previously described coordinate axes, and give a geometric account of how high-dimensional computation coexists with linear readout.

</details>


### [223] [Exemplar-based objective classification of gust-induced loads across multiple flight conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12448)
*Paolo Olivucci,Kowshik Srivatsan,David E. Rival*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习表征和摘要选择的客观分类准则，用于对gust-induced载荷进行分类。在3480个飞行翼模型的压力载荷测量数据上，该方法识别出9种跨越多个飞行姿态反复出现的基本响应类型，且分类结果保持物理可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有湍流（gust）引起的载荷分类通常依赖粗略参数（如飞行姿态）进行标记，缺乏客观且可解释的分类准则。作者希望找到一种既客观（基于相似性）又能保持物理可解释性的分类方法，以组织跨多个飞行条件下的复杂载荷数据。

Method: 该方法通过机器学习表征将大量实验观测编码为高维表示，然后应用摘要（summarization）程序选择最小子集的高度显著样本（exemplars）。这些exemplars提供基于相似性的客观分类准则，可供专家检查并进行更精细的实验。

Result: 在3480个随机gust引起的压力载荷测量数据（覆盖6种飞行姿态）上，该方法识别出9种在多个姿态间反复出现的基本响应类型。通过对各类型瞬态响应的分析，能够获得对底层流体力学机制的物理直觉。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了可以在保持可解释性的前提下实现客观的载荷分类，识别出的9种基本响应类型为理解gust-induced loads的流体力学机理提供了新视角，且显著样本可作为后续精细实验的研究对象。

Abstract: Is it possible to find an objective classification criterion that organizes the complexity of gust-induced loads across many flight conditions? And one that remains as interpretable as a labelling based on coarse parameters, such as the flight attitude? Our approach encodes a large number of experimental observations through a machine-learned representation and applies a summarization procedure to select a minimal subset of highly significant exemplars. The exemplars provide a similarity-based objective classification criterion of all the observations, they can be more conveniently inspected by experts and can become subject of more refined experiments. We demonstrate the approach on a database of 3480 pressure-load measurements induced by random gusts on a flying-wing model across six flight attitudes. We find nine fundamental response types that recur across multiple attitudes; analysis of a type's transient response enables physical intuition into the underlying fluid mechanics.

</details>


### [224] [Learning Under Treatment-Induced Label Indeterminacy with Expert Annotations of Counterfactual Outcomes: A Case Study in Neurological Prognostication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12477)
*Xiaobin Shen,Chloe Y. H. Huang,Jonathan Elmer,George H. Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以心脏骤停后神经预后预测为案例，指出当治疗决策使得临床相关结局对部分患者永久不可观察时，传统预测模型评估方式会掩盖重要缺陷。作者将患者划分为结局确定（certain）与不确定（uncertain）两类，提出显式分割评估框架及一个可利用两类标签进行权衡的预测模型，揭示标准评估指标（如AUROC）无法捕捉的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床预测模型通常假设每个患者的结局都能被干净地观察到，但当治疗决策使临床相关结局对部分患者永久不可观测时，这一假设失效。传统评估指标（如AUROC）在标准评估下表现相似的模型，可能在不确定病例上存在显著差异，而这些不确定性病例恰恰是最需要预后支持的群体。因此需要一个新的评估与建模框架来揭示并处理这一问题。

Method: 作者使用2,497名心脏骤停患者队列（其中1,429名患者结局因治疗决策而变不确定），由独立临床专家对这些不确定病例提供反事实结局猜测。提出一个显式将评估分割为确定病例与不确定病例的框架；并构建一个简单预测模型，能在两类标签之间进行权衡以利用双源标签信息。

Result: 在所提出的神经模型与多种表格基线模型中，具有相似确定病例AUROC的模型在确定病例Brier分数和对不确定病例的概率估计上可能差异显著。提高与不确定病例目标标签的匹配度通常以降低确定病例预测准确性为代价，凸显了标准评估所隐藏的显式权衡。

Conclusion: 当治疗决策决定临床有意义结局是否保持可观测性时，传统评估指标可能遗漏对最需要临床决策支持的患者的重大失败模式。因此，预测模型的评估必须考虑治疗决策对结局可观测性的影响，并显式区分确定与不确定病例。

Abstract: Clinical prediction models are often developed as if the outcome of interest were cleanly observed for every patient. This assumption fails when treatment decisions make the clinically relevant outcome permanently unobservable. As a case study of this problem, we consider post-cardiac-arrest neurological prognostication using a cohort of 2,497 patients, including 1,429 patients whose outcomes were rendered indeterminate by treatment decisions. These patients with indeterminate outcomes were reviewed by independent clinical experts, who provided their guesses of counterfactual outcomes about what would have happened to the patients. We refer to these patients as uncertain cases. We also have patients for whom we observe their clinically relevant outcomes; we refer to these patients as certain cases. We propose a framework for evaluating prediction models that explicitly splits the evaluation between certain and uncertain cases. Here, we cannot easily evaluate both types of cases in a uniform manner as the available target labels differ. We then propose a simple prediction model that uses target labels from both certain and uncertain cases in a manner that allows us to trade off between them. Across the proposed neural model and a collection of tabular baselines, models with similar certain-case AUROC can nevertheless differ substantially in both certain-case Brier score and their probability estimates for uncertain cases. Improving alignment with target labels of uncertain cases for our proposed model generally comes at the cost of worse accuracy on certain cases, highlighting an explicit tradeoff that standard evaluation conceals. These results show that when treatment decisions determine whether clinically meaningful outcomes remain observable, conventional evaluation metrics can miss important failure modes in the very patients for whom prognostic support matters most.

</details>


### [225] [When Can You Trust Offline Evaluation of Equal-Cost Top-k Allocation? A Controlled, Reproducible Benchmark and Practitioner's Guide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12489)
*Binshuang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对离线策略评估（OPE）中的确定性top-k目标规则进行了系统的基准测试，比较了六种估计器在五个数据集和两种已知效应扫描上的表现，并使用非模拟配对参考验证了机制。研究发现三个关键机制：弱重叠由logger-目标行为对齐而非logging锐度决定；优化器诅咒不能通过交叉拟合结果扰动项解决，需要诚实的策略级拆分；倾向性估计误差是所有压力测试中最大的退化来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织在预算约束下决定谁应该被治疗，并希望在使用目标规则之前知道该规则本可以赚多少钱。离线策略评估（OPE）承诺从记录的数据中实现这一点，但可部署的规则是确定性top-k策略，它消除了所有关于行为的平均，因此弱重叠会直接冲击估计量。需要系统地理解这些估计量在实际部署条件下的失败机制。

Method: 基准测试六种估计器，涵盖五个数据集和两次已知效应扫描。使用非模拟配对参考验证机制。分析三个具体机制：(1) 弱重叠与logger-目标行为对齐的关系；(2) 优化器诅咒与交叉拟合结果扰动项的关系；(3) 倾向性估计误差对IPS的影响。日志为合成生成，倾向性下限0.02以保证权重有界。

Result: (1) 弱重叠由logger-目标行为对齐决定，而非logging锐度；从目标自身分数构建的logger锐化几乎不改变重叠，行为级别的分歧会使其崩溃。有效样本量在跨日志环境间能排序风险，但在单个日志内排序候选的能力较弱且切点不迁移。(2) 交叉拟合结果扰动项无法修复优化器诅咒，反而使其更糟；诚实的策略级拆分通过改变估计目标避免了重用偏差。(3) 倾向性估计误差是最大的退化来源：out-of-fold估计对IPS的伤害超过任何其他压力测试，对双重稳健估计几乎无影响，但可以反转重叠诊断本身。倾向性下限0.02将两个调优混合估计量降为其未调优父版本，留下四个实际不同的估计量。所有精确值表面都是合成或半合成的。

Conclusion: 离线策略评估在确定性top-k部署策略下存在多个未被充分认识的风险机制：弱重叠的驱动因素是logger与目标行为之间的对齐而非logging锐度；优化器诅咒需要改变估计目标而非进行去偏；倾向性估计误差是最需要关注的退化来源。有效的样本量作为诊断工具在跨环境比较中有价值但在单日志候选排序中能力有限。基准测试已公开发布，仅使用公共数据。

Abstract: Organizations decide whom to treat under a budget and want to know what a targeting rule would have earned before deploying it. Off-policy evaluation promises this from logged data, but the deployable rule is a deterministic top-k policy: it removes all averaging over actions, so weak overlap hits the estimate directly. We benchmark six estimators across five datasets and two known-effect sweeps, and validate the mechanisms against a non-simulated paired reference. First, weak overlap is governed by logger-target action alignment, not by logging sharpness alone: what governs support is the logger's probability of the target's actions. Sharpening a logger built from the target's own score barely moves overlap over the tested range; action-level disagreement collapses it. Effective sample size ranks this risk across logging environments, but is weak at ranking candidates within the single log a practitioner holds, and its cut point does not transfer. Second, the optimizer's curse is not fixed by cross-fitting the outcome nuisance. When the rule is fit on the data used to evaluate it, cross-fitting the nuisance alone leaves the reuse bias in place and makes it worse. Honest policy-level splitting avoids the reuse by targeting the learning procedure's value -- a change of estimand, not a de-biasing of the full-sample policy. Third, propensity-estimation error is the largest degradation we measure: an out-of-fold estimate hurts IPS more than any other stress we apply, leaves doubly-robust estimation almost unchanged, and can invert the overlap diagnostic itself. Logging is synthesized and propensities floored at 0.02, so every failure occurs with bounded weights; the floor also reduces the two tuned hybrids to their untuned parents, leaving four practically distinct estimators, and all exact-value surfaces are synthetic or semi-synthetic. We release the benchmark; public data only.

</details>


### [226] [Exploring Oversmoothing with Householder Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12514)
*Bhaskar Karol*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HouseGNN，利用Householder反射器替代标准GCN的消息传递更新方式，设计逐片正交的GNN层以缓解过平滑问题，并理论证明其三层核心性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度图神经网络面临过平滑问题——随着网络深度增加，重复应用归一化图传播算子会导致节点表示向低信息子空间坍缩，限制了深层GNN的表达能力。标准GCN直接将邻居消息加到隐藏表示上，加剧了这一问题。

Method: HouseGNN不同于标准GCN直接更新隐藏状态，而是利用聚合的邻居消息仅估计一个反射方向，然后通过Householder反射器结合GroupSort激活函数来更新节点嵌入，形成逐片正交层，在每个节点和每个深度上保持欧几里得范数。

Result: 论文证明了三个核心性质：(i)每一内部层都保持节点级欧几里得范数；(ii)Householder反射器在消息的尺度和符号上具有不变性；(iii)节点间的成对距离可以通过节点级正交算子之间的不匹配而改变。

Conclusion: HouseGNN通过正交性设计从根本上避免过平滑导致的表示坍缩，同时保留节点间区分度（距离可变化），为深层GNN提供了一种新的架构范式。

Abstract: Deep graph neural networks(GNNs) suffer from oversmoothing- a progressive collapse of node representation towards a low information subspace as network depth increases because the normalized graph propagation operator is repeatedly applied directly to the hidden representations. In this work we study Householder Graph Neural Network (HouseGNN).
